</head>

<body>
    <!-- Header -->
    <div class="header" style="background-color: #1e293b;">
        <div class="logo">
            <span class="logo-icon"><i class="fas fa-lock"></i></span>
            <div>
                <div class="college-name">ICC Companion - Admin</div>
                <div class="college-short">ICON COMMERCE COLLEGE</div>
            </div>
        </div>
        <div class="user-info">
            <span class="user-name">Admin: <strong id="adminName">-</strong></span>
            <button class="btn-logout" onclick="logout('admin')">Logout</button>
        </div>
    </div>

    <!-- Navigation -->
    <nav class="nav-menu">
        <ul>
            <li><a href="dashboard.html"><i class="fas fa-house"></i> Dashboard</a></li>
            <li id="navStaff" style="display: none;"><a href="staff.html"><i class="fas fa-user-tie"></i> Manage Faculty</a></li>
            <li id="navDepts" style="display: none;"><a href="departments.html"><i class="fas fa-building"></i> Departments</a></li>
            <li><a href="students.html"><i class="fas fa-users"></i> Students</a></li>
            <li><a href="subjects.html"><i class="fas fa-book-open"></i> Subjects</a></li>

            <li><a href="exams.html"><i class="fas fa-calendar"></i> Exams</a></li>
            <li><a href="routines.html"><i class="fas fa-clipboard"></i> Routines</a></li>
            <li><a href="lost-found.html"><i class="fas fa-magnifying-glass"></i> Lost & Found</a></li>
            <li><a href="announcements.html" class="active"><i class="fas fa-bullhorn"></i> Announcements</a></li>
        </ul>
    </nav>

    <!-- Main Content -->
    <div class="container">
        <div style="display: flex; justify-content: space-between; align-items: center; margin-bottom: 20px;">
            <h1 class="page-title" style="margin-bottom: 0;"><i class="fas fa-bullhorn"></i> Manage Announcements</h1>
            <button class="btn btn-primary" onclick="showAddForm()">+ Post Announcement</button>
        </div>

        <!-- Announcements Table -->
        <div class="card">
            <div class="table-container">
                <table>
                    <thead>
                        <tr>
                            <th>Title</th>
                            <th>Priority</th>
                            <th>Target</th>
                            <th>Media</th>
                            <th>Date</th>
                            <th>Actions</th>
                        </tr>
                    </thead>
                    <tbody id="announcementsTable">
                        <tr>
                            <td colspan="6" style="text-align: center; padding: 40px; color: #64748b;">Loading...</td>
                        </tr>
                    </tbody>
                </table>
            </div>
        </div>
    </div>

    <!-- Add Announcement Modal -->
    <div id="announcementModal"
        style="display: none; position: fixed; top: 0; left: 0; right: 0; bottom: 0; background: rgba(0,0,0,0.5); z-index: 1000; align-items: center; justify-content: center; overflow-y: auto;">
        <div class="card" style="width: 90%; max-width: 550px; margin: 20px auto;">
            <h3 class="card-title">Post Announcement</h3>
            <form id="announcementForm" enctype="multipart/form-data">
                <input type="hidden" id="editId" value="">
                <div class="form-group">
                    <label for="title">Title *</label>
                    <input type="text" id="title" required placeholder="Announcement title">
                </div>

                <div class="form-group">
                    <label for="description">Description *</label>
                    <textarea id="description" rows="4" required placeholder="Announcement details..."></textarea>
                </div>

                <div class="form-row">
                    <div class="form-group">
                        <label for="priority">Priority *</label>
                        <select id="priority" required>
                            <option value="low">Low (Normal)</option>
                            <option value="medium" selected>Medium (Important)</option>
                            <option value="high">High (Urgent)</option>

    <script src="../js/main.js"></script>
    <script>
        if (!checkAdminLogin()) { }

        var admin = getAdminInfo();
        updateHeaderUI();

        // Show staff management if super-admin
        if (admin.role === 'super-admin') {
            document.getElementById('navStaff').style.display = 'block';
            document.getElementById('navDepts').style.display = 'block';
        }

        // Apply restrictions for assigned staff
        applyRestrictions('targetDept', 'targetSem');

        loadAnnouncements();

        function loadAnnouncements() {
            fetch('../api/get-announcements.php')
                .then(function (res) { return res.json(); })
                .then(function (data) {
                    var tbody = document.getElementById('announcementsTable');

                    if (data.success && data.data.length > 0) {
                        var html = '';
                        data.data.forEach(function (a) {
                            var priorityClass = a.priority === 'high' ? 'badge-danger' : (a.priority === 'medium' ? 'badge-warning' : 'badge-secondary');
                            var target = (a.target_dept === 'all' ? 'All Depts' : a.target_dept) + ' / ' + (a.target_semester === 'all' ? 'All Sem' : 'Sem ' + a.target_semester);

                            html += '<tr>';
                            html += '<td><strong>' + a.title + '</strong><br><small style="color:#64748b;">' + a.description.substring(0, 50) + '...</small></td>';
                            html += '<td><span class="badge ' + priorityClass + '">' + a.priority.toUpperCase() + '</span></td>';
                            html += '<td><span style="font-size: 12px;">' + target + '</span></td>';
                            html += '<td>';
                            if (a.attachment_url || a.link_url) {
                                if (a.attachment_url) {
                                    html += '<a href="../' + a.attachment_url + '" target="_blank" class="btn btn-sm btn-outline" title="View Attachment"><i class="fas fa-paperclip"></i> File</a> ';
                                }
                                if (a.link_url) {
                                    var displayUrl = a.link_url.replace('https://', '').replace('http://', '');
                                    if (displayUrl.length > 25) displayUrl = displayUrl.substring(0, 22) + '...';
                                    html += '<a href="' + a.link_url + '" target="_blank" class="btn btn-sm btn-outline"><i class="fas fa-link"></i> ' + displayUrl + '</a>';
                                }
                            } else {
                                html += '<span style="color: #94a3b8;">-</span>';
                            }
                            html += '</td>';
                            html += '<td>' + formatDate(a.created_at) + '</td>';
                            html += '<td>';
                            html += '<button class="btn btn-sm btn-outline" onclick=\'editAnnouncement(' + JSON.stringify(a) + ')\'>Edit</button> ';
                            html += '<button class="btn btn-sm btn-danger" onclick="deleteAnnouncement(' + a.announcement_id + ')">Delete</button>';
                            html += '</td>';
                            html += '</tr>';
                        });
                        tbody.innerHTML = html;
                    } else {
                        tbody.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="6" style="text-align: center; padding: 40px; color: #64748b;">No announcements</td></tr>';
                    }
                });
        }

        function showAddForm() {
            document.getElementById('editId').value = '';
            document.getElementById('announcementForm').reset();
            applyRestrictions('targetDept', 'targetSem');
            document.getElementById('announcementModal').style.display = 'flex';
        }

        function editAnnouncement(a) {
            document.getElementById('editId').value = a.announcement_id;
            document.getElementById('title').value = a.title;
            document.getElementById('description').value = a.description;
            document.getElementById('priority').value = a.priority;
            document.getElementById('targetDept').value = a.target_dept;
            document.getElementById('targetSem').value = a.target_semester;
            document.getElementById('link_url').value = a.link_url || '';

            // Re-apply restrictions if any
            applyRestrictions('targetDept', 'targetSem');

            document.getElementById('announcementModal').style.display = 'flex';
        }

        function closeModal() {
            document.getElementById('announcementModal').style.display = 'none';
        }

        function deleteAnnouncement(id) {
            if (!confirm('Delete this announcement?')) return;

            var formData = new FormData();
            formData.append('announcement_id', id);

            fetch('../api/delete-announcement.php', {
                method: 'POST',
                body: formData
            })
                .then(function (res) { return res.json(); })
                .then(function (data) {
                    if (data.success) {
                        showAlert('Announcement deleted', 'success');
                        loadAnnouncements();
                    }
                });
        }

        document.getElementById('announcementForm').addEventListener('submit', function (e) {
            e.preventDefault();

            var formData = new FormData();
            formData.append('title', document.getElementById('title').value);
            formData.append('description', document.getElementById('description').value);
            formData.append('priority', document.getElementById('priority').value);
            formData.append('target_dept', document.getElementById('targetDept').value);
            formData.append('target_semester', document.getElementById('targetSem').value);
            formData.append('link_url', document.getElementById('link_url').value);
            formData.append('posted_by', 'admin');

            // Add attachment if selected
            var attachmentFile = document.getElementById('attachment').files[0];
            if (attachmentFile) {
                formData.append('attachment', attachmentFile);
            }

            var editId = document.getElementById('editId').value;
            if (editId) {
                formData.append('announcement_id', editId);
            }

            var url = editId ? '../api/update-announcement.php' : '../api/add-announcement.php';

            fetch(url, {
                method: 'POST',
                body: formData
            })
                .then(function (res) { return res.json(); })
                .then(function (data) {
                    if (data.success) {
                        showAlert('Announcement posted', 'success');
                        closeModal();
                        loadAnnouncements();
                    } else {
                        showAlert(data.message, 'error');
                    }
                });
        });
    </script>
</body>

</html>

    <!-- Navigation -->
    <nav class="nav-menu">

            <li id="navDepts" style="display: none;"><a href="departments.html"><i class="fas fa-building"></i> Departments</a></li>
            <li><a href="students.html"><i class="fas fa-users"></i> Students</a></li>

            <li><a href="lost-found.html"><i class="fas fa-magnifying-glass"></i> Lost & Found</a></li>
            <li><a href="announcements.html"><i class="fas fa-bullhorn"></i> Announcements</a></li>
        </ul>
    </nav>

    <!-- Main Content -->
    <div class="container">
        <h1 class="page-title"><i class="fas fa-chart-bar"></i> Attendance Management</h1>

        <!-- Tabs -->
        <div class="tab-container">
            <button class="tab-btn active" onclick="showTab('mark')"><i class="fas fa-pen-to-square"></i> Mark Attendance</button>
            <button class="tab-btn" onclick="showTab('records')"><i class="fas fa-clipboard"></i> View Records</button>
        </div>

        <!-- TAB 1: MARK ATTENDANCE -->
        <div id="markTab">
            <!-- Select Class -->
            <div class="card">
                <h3 class="card-title">Select Class</h3>
                <div style="display: flex; gap: 15px; flex-wrap: wrap;">
                    <div class="form-group" style="margin: 0; min-width: 120px;">
                        <label>Department</label>
                        <select id="markDept" data-allow-all="true" onchange="updateSemesterDropdown('markDept', 'markSem'); loadMarkSubjects()">
                            <option value="">Select Dept</option>
                        </select>
                    </div>
                    <div class="form-group" style="margin: 0; min-width: 100px;">
                        <label>Semester</label>
                        <select id="markSem" data-allow-all="true" onchange="loadMarkSubjects()">
                            <option value="all">All</option>
                        </select>
                    </div>
                    <div class="form-group" style="margin: 0; min-width: 180px;">
                        <label>Subject</label>
                        <select id="markSubject" onchange="loadStudentsToMark()">
                            <option value="">Select dept & sem first</option>
                        </select>
                    </div>
                    <div class="form-group" style="margin: 0; min-width: 140px;">

                        <input type="date" id="markDate" onchange="loadStudentsToMark()">

            <!-- Students List -->
            <div class="card" id="markStudentsCard" style="display: none;">
                <div style="display: flex; justify-content: space-between; align-items: center; margin-bottom: 15px;">
                    <h3 style="margin: 0;">Mark Present Students</h3>
                    <div>
                        <button class="btn btn-sm btn-outline" onclick="selectAll()">Select All</button>
                        <button class="btn btn-sm btn-outline" onclick="deselectAll()">Deselect All</button>
                    </div>
                </div>
                <div class="scrollable-list-container">
                    <div class="checkbox-list" id="markStudentsList"></div>

                    <div style="display: flex; gap: 10px; align-items: center;">
                        <button class="btn btn-success" onclick="saveAttendance()">💾 Save Attendance</button>
                        <span id="selectedCount" style="color: #64748b;">0 selected</span>
                    </div>
                    <div id="saveStatus" style="margin-top: 10px; color: #22c55e; font-weight: 500; display: none;">✅ Attendance saved!</div>
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>

        <!-- TAB 2: VIEW RECORDS -->

                            <label>Dept</label>
                            <select id="viewDept" onchange="updateSemesterDropdown('viewDept', 'viewSem'); loadViewStudents()">
                                <option value="">Loading...</option>

    <script src="../js/main.js"></script>
    <script>
        if (!checkAdminLogin()) { }
        var admin = getAdminInfo();
        updateHeaderUI();

        // Show staff management if super-admin
        if (admin.role === 'super-admin') {
            document.getElementById('navStaff').style.display = 'block';
            document.getElementById('navDepts').style.display = 'block';
        }

        // Populate Depts
        populateDepartments('markDept');
        populateDepartments('viewDept');

        // Apply restrictions for assigned staff
        applyRestrictions('markDept', 'markSem');
        applyRestrictions('viewDept', 'viewSem');
        document.getElementById('markDate').value = new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0];
        document.getElementById('filterMonth').value = '';

        var markStudents = [];

        function showTab(tab) {
            document.querySelectorAll('.tab-btn').forEach(function (b) { b.classList.remove('active'); });
            event.target.classList.add('active');
            document.getElementById('markTab').style.display = tab === 'mark' ? 'block' : 'none';
            document.getElementById('recordsTab').style.display = tab === 'records' ? 'block' : 'none';
        }

        // ========== MARK ATTENDANCE TAB ==========
        function loadMarkSubjects() {
            var d = document.getElementById('markDept').value;
            var s = document.getElementById('markSem').value;
            // For faculty/super-admin, we allow empty filters to see all assigned subjects
            if (admin.role !== 'super-admin' && admin.role !== 'faculty' && (!d || !s)) return;
            fetch('../api/get-subjects.php?dept=' + d + '&semester=' + s)
                .then(function (r) { return r.json(); })
                .then(function (data) {
                    var html = '<option value="">Select</option>';
                    if (data.success) {
                        data.data.forEach(function (sub) { 
                            html += '<option value="' + sub.subject_id + '">' + sub.subject_name + '</option>'; 
                        });
                    }
                    var subjEl = document.getElementById('markSubject');
                    subjEl.innerHTML = html;

                    // Faculty Auto-selection & Locking
                    if (admin.role === 'faculty' && data.success && data.data.length > 0) {
                        subjEl.value = data.data[0].subject_id;
                        if (data.data.length === 1) {
                            subjEl.disabled = true;
                            subjEl.style.backgroundColor = '#f1f5f9';
                        }
                        // Trigger students load
                        loadStudentsToMark();
                    }
                });
        }

        function loadStudentsToMark() {
            var d = document.getElementById('markDept').value;
            var s = document.getElementById('markSem').value;
            var sub = document.getElementById('markSubject').value;
            var date = document.getElementById('markDate').value;
            if (!d || !s || !sub || !date) { document.getElementById('markStudentsCard').style.display = 'none'; return; }

            fetch('../api/get-students.php?dept=' + d + '&semester=' + s)
                .then(function (r) { return r.json(); })
                .then(function (data) {
                    if (data.success && data.data.length > 0) {
                        markStudents = data.data;
                        fetch('../api/get-attendance-by-date.php?subject_id=' + sub + '&date=' + date)
                            .then(function (r) { return r.json(); })
                            .then(function (att) {
                                var presentRolls = [];
                                if (att.success) att.data.forEach(function (a) { if (a.status === 'present') presentRolls.push(a.roll_number); });
                                renderMarkStudents(presentRolls);
                            });
                    }
                    document.getElementById('markStudentsCard').style.display = 'block';
                });
        }

        function renderMarkStudents(presentRolls) {
            var html = '';
            markStudents.forEach(function (s) {
                var checked = presentRolls.includes(s.roll_number) ? 'checked' : '';
                html += '<div class="checkbox-item"><input type="checkbox" id="st_' + s.roll_number + '" value="' + s.roll_number + '" ' + checked + ' onchange="updateCount()">';
                html += '<div class="student-info"><div class="roll">' + s.roll_number + '</div><div class="name">' + s.name + '</div></div></div>';
            });
            document.getElementById('markStudentsList').innerHTML = html;
            updateCount();
        }

        function updateCount() {
            var count = document.querySelectorAll('#markStudentsList input:checked').length;
            document.getElementById('selectedCount').textContent = count + ' of ' + markStudents.length + ' present';
        }

        function selectAll() { document.querySelectorAll('#markStudentsList input').forEach(function (c) { c.checked = true; }); updateCount(); }
        function deselectAll() { document.querySelectorAll('#markStudentsList input').forEach(function (c) { c.checked = false; }); updateCount(); }

        function saveAttendance() {
            var sub = document.getElementById('markSubject').value;
            var date = document.getElementById('markDate').value;
            var attData = [];
            markStudents.forEach(function (s) {
                var cb = document.getElementById('st_' + s.roll_number);
                attData.push({ roll_number: s.roll_number, status: cb.checked ? 'present' : 'absent' });
            });

            var fd = new FormData();
            fd.append('subject_id', sub);
            fd.append('date', date);
            fd.append('attendance', JSON.stringify(attData));
            fd.append('marked_by', admin.name);

            fetch('../api/save-attendance.php', { method: 'POST', body: fd })
                .then(function (r) { return r.json(); })
                .then(function (d) {
                    if (d.success) {
                        showAlert('Attendance saved!', 'success');
                        
                        // Show message below button
                        var statusMsg = document.getElementById('saveStatus');
                        statusMsg.style.display = 'block';
                        setTimeout(function() { statusMsg.style.display = 'none'; }, 3000);

                        // Refetch the updated records to show the current state
                        loadStudentsToMark();
                    }
                });
        }

        // ========== VIEW RECORDS TAB (SIMPLIFIED) ==========
        function loadViewStudents() {
            var d = document.getElementById('viewDept').value;
            var s = document.getElementById('viewSem').value;
            var studentSelect = document.getElementById('viewStudent');

            if (!d || !s) {
                studentSelect.innerHTML = '<option value="">-- All Students --</option>';
                return;
            }

            fetch('../api/get-students.php?dept=' + d + '&semester=' + s)
                .then(function (r) { return r.json(); })
                .then(function (data) {
                    var html = '<option value="">-- All Students --</option>';
                    if (data.success) {
                        data.data.forEach(function (st) {
                            html += '<option value="' + st.roll_number + '">' + st.roll_number + ' - ' + st.name + '</option>';
                        });
                    }
                    studentSelect.innerHTML = html;
                });
        }

        function loadViewStudents() {
            var d = document.getElementById('viewDept').value;
            var s = document.getElementById('viewSem').value;
            if (!d || !s) return;

            fetch('../api/get-students.php?dept=' + d + '&semester=' + s)
                .then(function (r) { return r.json(); })
                .then(function (data) {
                    var html = '<option value="">-- All Students --</option>';
                    if (data.success) {
                        data.data.forEach(function (st) {
                            html += '<option value="' + st.roll_number + '">' + st.roll_number + ' - ' + st.name + '</option>';
                        });
                    }
                    document.getElementById('viewStudent').innerHTML = html;
                });
        }

        function viewAttendance() {
            var d = document.getElementById('viewDept').value;
            var s = document.getElementById('viewSem').value;
            var roll = document.getElementById('viewStudent').value;
            var month = document.getElementById('filterMonth').value;
            var container = document.getElementById('viewResults');

            if (!d || !s) {
                container.innerHTML = '<div class="alert alert-warning">Please select Department and Semester</div>';
                return;
            }

            var url = '../api/get-attendance-summary.php?dept=' + d + '&semester=' + s;
            if (roll) url += '&roll_number=' + roll;
            if (month) url += '&month=' + month;

            fetch(url)
                .then(function (r) { return r.json(); })
                .then(function (data) {
                    if (!data.success || data.data.length === 0) {
                        container.innerHTML = '<div class="card" style="text-align: center; padding: 40px; color: #64748b;">No attendance records found</div>';
                        return;
                    }

                    if (roll) {
                        // SINGLE STUDENT - Show subject-wise breakdown
                        renderStudentReport(data.data, roll, container);
                    } else {
                        // ALL STUDENTS - Show overview list
                        renderAllStudents(data.data, container);
                    }
                });
        }

        function renderStudentReport(data, rollNumber, container) {
            // Group by subject and calculate totals
            var studentName = data[0].student_name;
            var totalPresent = 0, totalClasses = 0;

            var html = '<div class="card">';
            html += '<div style="display: flex; justify-content: space-between; align-items: center; margin-bottom: 20px;">';
            html += '<div>';
            html += '<h3 style="margin: 0;"><i class="fas fa-chart-bar"></i> ' + rollNumber + '</h3>';
            html += '<div style="color: #64748b; font-size: 14px;">' + studentName + ' | ' + data[0].dept_code + ' - Sem ' + data[0].semester + '</div>';
            html += '</div>';
            html += '</div>';

            html += '<table style="width: 100%;">';
            html += '<thead><tr><th style="text-align: left;">Subject</th><th style="text-align: center;">Total</th><th style="text-align: center;">Present</th><th style="text-align: right;">Percentage</th></tr></thead>';
            html += '<tbody>';

            data.forEach(function (r) {
                var pct = r.total_classes > 0 ? Math.round((r.present_count / r.total_classes) * 100) : 0;
                var color = pct >= 75 ? '#22c55e' : (pct >= 50 ? '#f59e0b' : '#ef4444');
                totalPresent += parseInt(r.present_count);
                totalClasses += parseInt(r.total_classes);

                html += '<tr style="border-bottom: 1px solid #e2e8f0;">';
                html += '<td style="padding: 12px 0;">' + r.subject_name + '</td>';
                html += '<td style="text-align: center;">' + r.total_classes + '</td>';
                html += '<td style="text-align: center;">' + r.present_count + '</td>';
                html += '<td style="text-align: right;"><span style="font-weight: 600; color: ' + color + ';">' + pct + '%</span></td>';
                html += '</tr>';
            });

            // Overall
            var overallPct = totalClasses > 0 ? Math.round((totalPresent / totalClasses) * 100) : 0;
            var overallColor = overallPct >= 75 ? '#22c55e' : (overallPct >= 50 ? '#f59e0b' : '#ef4444');

            html += '</tbody>';
            html += '<tfoot><tr style="background: #f8fafc; font-weight: bold;">';
            html += '<td style="padding: 12px 0;">OVERALL</td>';
            html += '<td style="text-align: center;">' + totalClasses + '</td>';
            html += '<td style="text-align: center;">' + totalPresent + '</td>';
            html += '<td style="text-align: right;"><span style="font-size: 18px; color: ' + overallColor + ';">' + overallPct + '%</span></td>';
            html += '</tr></tfoot>';
            html += '</table></div>';

            container.innerHTML = html;
        }

        function renderAllStudents(data, container) {
            // Group by student and calculate overall attendance
            var students = {};
            data.forEach(function (r) {
                if (!students[r.roll_number]) {
                    students[r.roll_number] = { name: r.student_name, present: 0, total: 0 };
                }
                students[r.roll_number].present += parseInt(r.present_count);
                students[r.roll_number].total += parseInt(r.total_classes);
            });

            var html = '<div class="card">';
            html += '<h3 class="card-title">Attendance Overview</h3>';
            html += '<table style="width: 100%;"><thead><tr>';
            html += '<th style="text-align: left;">Roll No</th>';
            html += '<th style="text-align: left;">Student Name</th>';
            html += '<th style="text-align: center;">Present / Total</th>';
            html += '<th style="text-align: right; min-width: 150px;">Attendance</th>';
            html += '</tr></thead><tbody>';

            Object.keys(students).forEach(function (roll) {
                var st = students[roll];
                var pct = st.total > 0 ? Math.round((st.present / st.total) * 100) : 0;
                var color = pct >= 75 ? '#22c55e' : (pct >= 50 ? '#f59e0b' : '#ef4444');
                var barClass = pct >= 75 ? 'good' : (pct >= 50 ? 'warning' : 'danger');

                html += '<tr style="border-bottom: 1px solid #e2e8f0;">';
                html += '<td style="padding: 10px 0;"><strong>' + roll + '</strong></td>';
                html += '<td>' + st.name + '</td>';
                html += '<td style="text-align: center;">' + st.present + ' / ' + st.total + '</td>';
                html += '<td style="text-align: right;">';
                html += '<div style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 10px;">';
                html += '<div class="percentage-bar" style="flex: 1;"><div class="fill ' + barClass + '" style="width: ' + pct + '%;"></div></div>';
                html += '<span style="font-weight: 600; color: ' + color + '; min-width: 45px;">' + pct + '%</span>';
                html += '</div></td>';
                html += '</tr>';
            });

            html += '</tbody></table></div>';
            container.innerHTML = html;
        }
    </script>
</body>

</html>

    <script>
        if (!checkAdminLogin()) {}

        var admin = getAdminInfo();
        updateHeaderUI();
        document.getElementById('welcomeName').textContent = admin.name;

        var roleLabel, roleClass, scopeText, welcomeClass, sectionLabel;
        var dept = admin.assignedDept === 'all' ? 'All Departments' : admin.assignedDept;
        var sem = admin.assignedSemester === 'all' ? 'All Semesters' : 'Semester ' + admin.assignedSemester;

        if (admin.role === 'super-admin') {
            roleLabel = 'PRINCIPAL';
            roleClass = 'principal';
            scopeText = 'You have full system access to all modules.';
            sectionLabel = 'Full Access';
            welcomeClass = 'principal';
            document.getElementById('navStaff').style.display = 'block';
            document.getElementById('navDepts').style.display = 'block';
            document.getElementById('cardStaff').style.display = 'block';
            document.getElementById('cardDepts').style.display = 'block';
        } else if (admin.role === 'dept-admin') {
            roleLabel = 'HOD';
            roleClass = 'hod';
            scopeText = 'Managing <strong>' + dept + '</strong> department.';
            sectionLabel = 'Department Tools';
            welcomeClass = 'hod';
            ['Depts', 'Staff'].forEach(function(item) {
                var nav = document.getElementById('nav' + item);
                var card = document.getElementById('card' + item);
                if (nav) nav.style.display = 'none';
                if (card) card.style.display = 'none';
            });
        } else {
            roleLabel = 'FACULTY';
            roleClass = 'faculty';
            scopeText = 'Assigned: <strong>' + dept + '</strong> | <strong>' + sem + '</strong>';
            sectionLabel = 'My Tools';
            welcomeClass = 'faculty';
            ['Staff', 'Depts', 'Subjects', 'Exams', 'Routines', 'LostFound', 'Announcements'].forEach(function(item) {
                var nav = document.getElementById('nav' + item);
                var card = document.getElementById('card' + item);
                if (nav) nav.style.display = 'none';
                if (card) card.style.display = 'none';
            });
            document.getElementById('facultySubjectsSection').style.display = 'block';
            document.getElementById('deptStatsSection').style.display = 'none';
            loadFacultySubjects();
        }

        document.getElementById('roleBadge').className = 'role-badge-lg ' + roleClass;
        document.getElementById('roleBadge').innerHTML = '<i class="fas fa-shield-halved"></i> ' + roleLabel;
        document.getElementById('scopeBadge').innerHTML = scopeText;
        document.getElementById('welcomeCard').classList.add(welcomeClass);
        document.getElementById('quickActionsTitle').textContent = 'Quick Actions \u2022 ' + sectionLabel;
        document.getElementById('quickActionsBadge').className = 'section-badge ' + roleClass;
        document.getElementById('quickActionsBadge').textContent = roleLabel;

        loadDashboardStats();

        function buildStatCards(cards) {
            var html = '';
            cards.forEach(function(c) {
                html += '<div class="stat-card">';
                html += '  <div class="stat-icon ' + c.color + '"><i class="' + c.icon + '"></i></div>';
                html += '  <div class="stat-info">';
                html += '    <h3 id="' + c.id + '">--</h3>';
                html += '    <p>' + c.label + '</p>';
                html += '  </div>';
                html += '</div>';
            });
            document.getElementById('statsContainer').innerHTML = html;
        }

        function loadDashboardStats() {
            if (admin.role === 'faculty') {
                buildStatCards([
                    { id: 'statStudents', label: 'My Students', icon: 'fas fa-users', color: 'blue' },
                    { id: 'statSubjects', label: 'My Subjects', icon: 'fas fa-book-open', color: 'purple' },
                    { id: 'statExams', label: 'Upcoming Exams', icon: 'fas fa-calendar', color: 'green' }
                ]);
            } else {
                buildStatCards([
                    { id: 'statStudents', label: admin.role === 'dept-admin' ? 'Dept Students' : 'Total Students', icon: 'fas fa-users', color: 'blue' },
                    { id: 'statExams', label: 'Upcoming Exams', icon: 'fas fa-calendar', color: 'green' },
                    { id: 'statLost', label: 'Active Lost/Found', icon: 'fas fa-magnifying-glass', color: 'orange' },
                    { id: 'statAnnounce', label: 'Announcements', icon: 'fas fa-bullhorn', color: 'red' }
                ]);
            }

            fetch('../api/get-students.php')
                .then(function(res) { return res.json(); })
                .then(function(data) {
                    if (data.success) {
                        var el = document.getElementById('statStudents');
                        if (el) el.textContent = data.data.length;

                        var deptHtml = '';
                        var deptCount = {};
                        data.data.forEach(function(s) { deptCount[s.dept_code] = (deptCount[s.dept_code] || 0) + 1; });
                        for (var d in deptCount) {
                            deptHtml += '<div style="text-align: center; padding: 20px; background: #f8fafc; border-radius: 8px;">';
                            deptHtml += '  <div style="font-size: 24px; font-weight: bold; color: var(--primary);">' + deptCount[d] + '</div>';
                            deptHtml += '  <div style="font-size: 14px; color: #64748b;">' + d + '</div>';
                            deptHtml += '</div>';
                        }
                        var ds = document.getElementById('deptStats');
                        if (ds) ds.innerHTML = deptHtml;
                    }
                });

            if (admin.role !== 'faculty') {
                fetch('../api/get-exams.php')
                    .then(function(res) { return res.json(); })
                    .then(function(data) { if (data.success) { var el = document.getElementById('statExams'); if (el) el.textContent = data.data.length; } });

                fetch('../api/get-lost-found.php?status=active')
                    .then(function(res) { return res.json(); })
                    .then(function(data) { if (data.success) { var el = document.getElementById('statLost'); if (el) el.textContent = data.data.length; } });

                fetch('../api/get-announcements.php')
                    .then(function(res) { return res.json(); })
                    .then(function(data) { if (data.success) { var el = document.getElementById('statAnnounce'); if (el) el.textContent = data.data.length; } });
            } else {
                fetch('../api/get-exams.php')
                    .then(function(res) { return res.json(); })
                    .then(function(data) { if (data.success) { var el = document.getElementById('statExams'); if (el) el.textContent = data.data.length; } });

                fetch('../api/get-faculty-subjects.php?admin_id=' + admin.id)
                    .then(function(res) { return res.json(); })
                    .then(function(data) { if (data.success) { var el = document.getElementById('statSubjects'); if (el) el.textContent = data.data.length; } });
            }
        }

        function loadFacultySubjects() {
            fetch('../api/get-faculty-subjects.php?admin_id=' + admin.id)
                .then(function(res) { return res.json(); })
                .then(function(data) {
                    var container = document.getElementById('facultySubjectsList');
                    if (data.success && data.data.length > 0) {
                        var html = '';
                        data.data.forEach(function(s) {
                            html += '<div class="subject-mini-card">';
                            html += '  <div class="subj-icon"><i class="fas fa-book"></i></div>';
                            html += '  <div class="subj-name">' + s.subject_name + ' (' + s.subject_code + ')</div>';
                            html += '  <div class="subj-meta">' + (s.dept_code || '') + ' | Sem ' + (s.semester || '') + '</div>';
                            html += '</div>';
                        });
                        container.innerHTML = html;
                    } else {
                        container.innerHTML = '<div style="text-align: center; padding: 20px; color: #64748b; grid-column: 1/-1;">No subjects assigned yet.</div>';
                    }
                });
        }
    </script>
</body>

</html>

                    <thead>
                        <tr>
                            <th>Code</th>
                            <th>Department Name</th>
                            <th>Max Semesters</th>
                            <th>Actions</th>
                        </tr>
                    </thead>
                    <tbody id="deptTable">
                        <tr>
                            <td colspan="4" style="text-align: center; padding: 40px; color: #64748b;">Loading departments...</td>
                        </tr>
                    </tbody>

    <script src="../js/main.js"></script>
    <script>
        // Check if logged in
        if (!checkAdminLogin()) { }

        // Get admin info
        var admin = getAdminInfo();
        updateHeaderUI();

        // Security: Only super-admin should be here
        if (admin.role !== 'super-admin') {
            alert('Access Denied: Super Admin only');
            window.location.href = 'dashboard.html';
        } else {
            document.getElementById('navStaff').style.display = 'block';
            document.getElementById('navDepts').style.display = 'block';
        }

        loadDepartments();

        function loadDepartments() {
            fetch('../api/get-departments.php')
                .then(function (res) { return res.json(); })
                .then(function (data) {
                    const tbody = document.getElementById('deptTable');
                    if (data.success && data.data.length > 0) {
                        let html = '';
                        data.data.forEach(dept => {
                            html += '<tr>';
                            html += '<td><span class="badge badge-info">' + dept.dept_code + '</span></td>';
                            html += '<td><strong>' + dept.dept_name + '</strong></td>';
                            html += '<td>' + dept.max_semesters + ' Semesters</td>';
                            html += '<td>';
                            html += '<button class="btn btn-sm btn-outline" onclick=\'editDept(' + JSON.stringify(dept) + ')\'>Edit</button> ';
                            html += '<button class="btn btn-sm btn-danger" onclick="deleteDept(' + dept.dept_id + ')">Delete</button>';
                            html += '</td>';
                            html += '</tr>';
                        });
                        tbody.innerHTML = html;
                    } else {
                        tbody.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="4" style="text-align: center; padding: 40px; color: #64748b;">No departments found.</td></tr>';
                    }
                });
        }

        function showAddModal() {
            document.getElementById('modalTitle').textContent = 'Add New Department';
            document.getElementById('deptForm').reset();
            document.getElementById('editDeptId').value = '';
            document.getElementById('deptModal').style.display = 'flex';
        }

        function closeModal() {
            document.getElementById('deptModal').style.display = 'none';
        }

        function editDept(dept) {
            document.getElementById('modalTitle').textContent = 'Edit Department';
            document.getElementById('editDeptId').value = dept.dept_id;
            document.getElementById('deptCode').value = dept.dept_code;
            document.getElementById('deptName').value = dept.dept_name;
            document.getElementById('maxSems').value = dept.max_semesters;
            document.getElementById('deptModal').style.display = 'flex';
        }

        function deleteDept(id) {
            if (confirm('Are you sure you want to delete this department? This may cause issues with associated data.')) {
                const fd = new FormData();
                fd.append('dept_id', id);
                fetch('../api/delete-department.php', { method: 'POST', body: fd })
                    .then(res => res.json())
                    .then(data => {
                        if (data.success) {
                            showAlert('Department deleted successfully', 'success');
                            loadDepartments();
                        } else {
                            showAlert(data.message, 'error');
                        }
                    });
            }
        }

        document.getElementById('deptForm').addEventListener('submit', function (e) {
            e.preventDefault();
            const id = document.getElementById('editDeptId').value;
            const fd = new FormData();
            fd.append('dept_code', document.getElementById('deptCode').value);
            fd.append('dept_name', document.getElementById('deptName').value);
            fd.append('max_semesters', document.getElementById('maxSems').value);

            let url = '../api/add-department.php';
            if (id) {
                fd.append('dept_id', id);
                url = '../api/update-department.php';
            }

            fetch(url, { method: 'POST', body: fd })
                .then(res => res.json())
                .then(data => {
                    if (data.success) {
                        showAlert(id ? 'Department updated' : 'Department added', 'success');
                        closeModal();
                        loadDepartments();
                    } else {
                        showAlert(data.message, 'error');
                    }
                });
        });
    </script>
</body>

</html>

        style="display: none; position: fixed; top: 0; left: 0; right: 0; bottom: 0; background: rgba(0,0,0,0.5); z-index: 1000; align-items: center; justify-content: center; overflow-y: auto;">

    <script src="../js/main.js"></script>
    <script>
        if (!checkAdminLogin()) { }
        var admin = getAdminInfo();
        updateHeaderUI();

        // Show staff management if super-admin
        if (admin.role === 'super-admin') {
            document.getElementById('navStaff').style.display = 'block';
            document.getElementById('navDepts').style.display = 'block';
        }

        // Populate dynamic Depts
        populateDepartments('sessionalDept');
        populateDepartments('finalDept');
        populateDepartments('dept');

        // Apply restrictions for assigned staff
        applyRestrictions('sessionalDept', 'sessionalSem');
        applyRestrictions('finalDept', 'finalSem');
        applyRestrictions('dept', 'semester'); // Modal

        // Load data
        loadExams();
        loadScheduleFiles();

        function loadExams() {
            fetch('../api/get-exams.php')
                .then(function (res) { return res.json(); })
                .then(function (data) {
                    if (data.success) {
                        var sessional = data.data.filter(function (e) { return e.exam_type === 'sessional'; });
                        var final = data.data.filter(function (e) { return e.exam_type === 'final'; });
                        renderExamTable('sessionalTable', sessional);
                        renderExamTable('finalTable', final);
                    }
                });
        }

        function renderExamTable(tableId, exams) {
            var tbody = document.getElementById(tableId);
            if (exams.length === 0) {
                tbody.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="7" style="text-align: center; color: #64748b; padding: 20px;">No exams added</td></tr>';
                return;
            }
            var html = '';
            exams.forEach(function (e) {
                html += '<tr>';
                html += '<td>' + formatDate(e.exam_date) + '</td>';
                html += '<td>' + e.subject_name + '</td>';
                html += '<td><span class="badge badge-info">' + e.dept_code + '</span></td>';
                html += '<td>' + e.semester + '</td>';
                html += '<td>' + formatTimeStr(e.start_time) + '-' + formatTimeStr(e.end_time) + '</td>';
                html += '<td>' + e.room + '</td>';
                html += '<td>';
                html += '<button class="btn btn-sm btn-outline" onclick=\'editExam(' + JSON.stringify(e) + ')\'>Edit</button> ';
                html += '<button class="btn btn-sm btn-danger" onclick="deleteExam(' + e.exam_id + ')">Del</button>';
                html += '</td>';
                html += '</tr>';
            });
            tbody.innerHTML = html;
        }

        function loadScheduleFiles() {
            fetch('../api/get-exam-schedules.php')
                .then(function (res) { return res.json(); })
                .then(function (data) {
                    if (data.success) {
                        var sessionalList = document.getElementById('sessionalFilesList');
                        var finalList = document.getElementById('finalFilesList');
                        sessionalList.innerHTML = '';
                        finalList.innerHTML = '';

                        var sessionalCount = 0;
                        var finalCount = 0;

                        data.data.forEach(function (s) {
                            var list = s.schedule_type === 'sessional' ? sessionalList : finalList;
                            if (s.schedule_type === 'sessional') sessionalCount++; else finalCount++;

                            var item = document.createElement('div');
                            item.style.background = '#fff';
                            item.style.padding = '5px 10px';
                            item.style.borderRadius = '5px';
                            item.style.border = '1px solid #e2e8f0';
                            item.style.display = 'flex';
                            item.style.alignItems = 'center';
                            item.style.gap = '10px';
                            item.style.fontSize = '12px';

                            item.innerHTML = '<span>' + s.dept_code + ' - S' + s.semester + '</span> ' +
                                '<a href="../' + s.file_url + '" target="_blank"><i class="fas fa-paperclip"></i> View</a> ' +
                                '<button class="btn btn-sm btn-danger" style="padding: 1px 5px; font-size: 10px;" ' +
                                'onclick="deleteSchedule(\'' + s.schedule_type + '\', \'' + s.dept_code + '\', ' + s.semester + ')"><i class="fas fa-trash-can"></i></button>';
                            list.appendChild(item);
                        });

                        if (sessionalCount === 0) sessionalList.innerHTML = '<span style="color: #64748b; font-size: 13px;">No files uploaded</span>';
                        if (finalCount === 0) finalList.innerHTML = '<span style="color: #64748b; font-size: 13px;">No files uploaded</span>';
                    }
                });
        }

        function uploadSchedule(type) {
            var fileInput = document.getElementById(type + 'File');
            var dept = document.getElementById(type + 'Dept').value;
            var sem = document.getElementById(type + 'Sem').value;
            if (!fileInput.files[0]) return;

            var formData = new FormData();
            formData.append('schedule_type', type);
            formData.append('dept_code', dept);
            formData.append('semester', sem);
            formData.append('file', fileInput.files[0]);

            fetch('../api/upload-exam-schedule.php', { method: 'POST', body: formData })
                .then(function (res) { return res.json(); })
                .then(function (data) {
                    if (data.success) {
                        showAlert('Schedule uploaded', 'success');
                        loadScheduleFiles();
                        fileInput.value = '';
                    } else {
                        showAlert(data.message, 'error');
                    }
                });
        }

        function deleteSchedule(type, dept, sem) {
            if (!confirm('Delete this schedule file?')) return;
            var formData = new FormData();
            formData.append('schedule_type', type);
            formData.append('dept_code', dept);
            formData.append('semester', sem);

            fetch('../api/delete-exam-schedule.php', { method: 'POST', body: formData })
                .then(function (res) { return res.json(); })
                .then(function (data) {
                    if (data.success) {
                        showAlert('Deleted', 'success');
                        loadScheduleFiles();
                    }
                });
        }

        function formatTimeStr(t) {
            var p = t.split(':'); var h = parseInt(p[0]); var ampm = h >= 12 ? 'PM' : 'AM';
            return (h % 12 || 12) + ':' + p[1] + ampm;
        }

        function loadSubjectOptions() {
            var d = document.getElementById('dept').value;
            var s = document.getElementById('semester').value;
            if (!d || !s) return;
            fetch('../api/get-subjects.php?dept=' + d + '&semester=' + s)
                .then(function (res) { return res.json(); })
                .then(function (data) {
                    var html = '<option value="">Select</option>';
                    if (data.success) data.data.forEach(function (s) { html += '<option value="' + s.subject_id + '">' + s.subject_name + '</option>'; });
                    document.getElementById('subject').innerHTML = html;
                });
        }

        function showAddForm(type) {
            document.getElementById('examType').value = type;
            document.getElementById('editId').value = '';
            document.getElementById('modalTitle').textContent = 'Add ' + (type === 'sessional' ? 'Sessional' : 'Final') + ' Exam';
            document.getElementById('examForm').reset();
            applyRestrictions('dept', 'semester');
            document.getElementById('examModal').style.display = 'flex';
        }

        function editExam(e) {
            document.getElementById('examType').value = e.exam_type;
            document.getElementById('editId').value = e.exam_id;
            document.getElementById('modalTitle').textContent = 'Edit ' + (e.exam_type === 'sessional' ? 'Sessional' : 'Final') + ' Exam';
            document.getElementById('examForm').reset();
            
            document.getElementById('dept').value = e.dept_code;
            document.getElementById('semester').value = e.semester;
            document.getElementById('examDate').value = e.exam_date;
            document.getElementById('room').value = e.room;
            document.getElementById('startTime').value = e.start_time;
            document.getElementById('endTime').value = e.end_time;
            
            // Re-apply restrictions
            applyRestrictions('dept', 'semester');
            
            loadSubjectOptions();
            setTimeout(() => {
                document.getElementById('subject').value = e.subject_id;
            }, 500);

            document.getElementById('examModal').style.display = 'flex';
        }

        function closeModal() { document.getElementById('examModal').style.display = 'none'; }

        function deleteExam(id) {
            if (!confirm('Delete?')) return;
            var fd = new FormData(); fd.append('exam_id', id);
            fetch('../api/delete-exam.php', { method: 'POST', body: fd })
                .then(function (r) { return r.json(); })
                .then(function (d) { if (d.success) { showAlert('Deleted', 'success'); loadExams(); } });
        }

        document.getElementById('examForm').addEventListener('submit', function (e) {
            e.preventDefault();
            var fd = new FormData();
            fd.append('subject_id', document.getElementById('subject').value);
            fd.append('exam_date', document.getElementById('examDate').value);
            fd.append('start_time', document.getElementById('startTime').value);
            fd.append('end_time', document.getElementById('endTime').value);
            fd.append('room', document.getElementById('room').value);
            fd.append('exam_type', document.getElementById('examType').value);

            var editId = document.getElementById('editId').value;
            if (editId) fd.append('exam_id', editId);
            
            var url = editId ? '../api/update-exam.php' : '../api/add-exam.php';

            fetch(url, { method: 'POST', body: fd })
                .then(function (r) { return r.json(); })
                .then(function (d) {
                    if (d.success) { showAlert('Added', 'success'); closeModal(); loadExams(); }
                    else showAlert(d.message, 'error');
                });
        });
    </script>
</body>

</html>

    <script>
        if (!checkAdminLogin()) { }

        var admin = getAdminInfo();
        updateHeaderUI();

        // Show staff management if super-admin
        if (admin.role === 'super-admin') {
            document.getElementById('navStaff').style.display = 'block';
            document.getElementById('navDepts').style.display = 'block';
        }

        document.getElementById('itemDate').value = new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0];

        loadItems();

        function loadItems() {
            fetch('../api/get-lost-found.php?status=')
                .then(function (res) { return res.json(); })
                .then(function (data) {
                    var tbody = document.getElementById('itemsTable');

                    if (data.success && data.data.length > 0) {
                        var html = '';
                        data.data.forEach(function (item) {
                            var typeClass = item.item_type === 'lost' ? 'badge-danger' : 'badge-success';
                            var statusClass = item.status === 'claimed' ? 'badge-secondary' : 'badge-info';

                            html += '<tr>';
                            // Image column
                            html += '<td style="width: 60px;">';
                            if (item.image_url) {
                                html += '<img src="../' + item.image_url + '" style="width: 50px; height: 50px; object-fit: cover; border-radius: 5px;">';
                            } else {
                                html += '<div style="width: 50px; height: 50px; background: #f1f5f9; border-radius: 5px; display: flex; align-items: center; justify-content: center; color: #94a3b8;">📷</div>';
                            }
                            html += '</td>';
                            html += '<td><strong>' + item.title + '</strong></td>';
                            html += '<td><span class="badge ' + typeClass + '">' + item.item_type.toUpperCase() + '</span></td>';
                            html += '<td>' + item.category + '</td>';
                            html += '<td>' + item.location + '</td>';
                            html += '<td>' + formatDate(item.item_date) + '</td>';
                            html += '<td><span class="badge ' + statusClass + '">' + item.status + '</span></td>';
                            html += '<td>';
                            if (item.status === 'active') {
                                html += '<button class="btn btn-sm btn-success" onclick="markClaimed(' + item.item_id + ')">Claimed</button> ';
                            }
                            html += '<button class="btn btn-sm btn-outline" onclick=\'editItem(' + JSON.stringify(item) + ')\'>Edit</button> ';
                            html += '<button class="btn btn-sm btn-danger" onclick="deleteItem(' + item.item_id + ')">Delete</button>';
                            html += '</td>';
                            html += '</tr>';
                        });
                        tbody.innerHTML = html;
                    } else {
                        tbody.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="8" style="text-align: center; padding: 40px; color: #64748b;">No items found</td></tr>';
                    }
                });
        }

        function showAddForm() {
            document.getElementById('editId').value = '';
            document.getElementById('itemForm').reset();
            document.getElementById('submitBtn').textContent = 'Save Item';
            document.getElementById('itemDate').value = new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0];
            document.getElementById('itemModal').style.display = 'flex';
        }

        function editItem(item) {
            document.getElementById('editId').value = item.item_id;
            document.getElementById('title').value = item.title;
            document.getElementById('itemType').value = item.item_type;
            document.getElementById('category').value = item.category;
            document.getElementById('itemDate').value = item.item_date;
            document.getElementById('location').value = item.location;
            document.getElementById('description').value = item.description;
            document.getElementById('contactInfo').value = item.contact_info;
            document.getElementById('submitBtn').textContent = 'Update Item';
            document.getElementById('itemModal').style.display = 'flex';
        }

        function closeModal() {
            document.getElementById('itemModal').style.display = 'none';
        }

        function markClaimed(id) {
            if (!confirm('Mark this item as claimed?')) return;

            var formData = new FormData();
            formData.append('item_id', id);
            formData.append('status', 'claimed');

            fetch('../api/update-lost-found.php', {
                method: 'POST',
                body: formData
            })
                .then(function (res) { return res.json(); })
                .then(function (data) {
                    if (data.success) {
                        showAlert('Item marked as claimed', 'success');
                        loadItems();
                    }
                });
        }

        function deleteItem(id) {
            if (!confirm('Delete this item?')) return;

            var formData = new FormData();
            formData.append('item_id', id);

            fetch('../api/delete-lost-found.php', {
                method: 'POST',
                body: formData
            })
                .then(function (res) { return res.json(); })
                .then(function (data) {
                    if (data.success) {
                        showAlert('Item deleted', 'success');
                        loadItems();
                    }
                });
        }

        document.getElementById('itemForm').addEventListener('submit', function (e) {
            e.preventDefault();

            var formData = new FormData();
            formData.append('title', document.getElementById('title').value);
            formData.append('item_type', document.getElementById('itemType').value);
            formData.append('category', document.getElementById('category').value);
            formData.append('item_date', document.getElementById('itemDate').value);
            formData.append('location', document.getElementById('location').value);
            formData.append('description', document.getElementById('description').value);
            formData.append('contact_info', document.getElementById('contactInfo').value);
            formData.append('posted_by', 'admin');

            // Add image if selected
            var imageFile = document.getElementById('itemImage').files[0];
            if (imageFile) {
                formData.append('image', imageFile);
            }

            var editId = document.getElementById('editId').value;
            if (editId) {
                formData.append('item_id', editId);
            }

            var url = editId ? '../api/update-lost-found.php' : '../api/add-lost-found.php';

            fetch(url, {
                method: 'POST',
                body: formData
            })
                .then(function (res) { return res.json(); })
                .then(function (data) {
                    if (data.success) {
                        showAlert('Item added', 'success');
                        closeModal();
                        loadItems();
                    } else {
                        showAlert(data.message, 'error');
                    }
                });
        });
    </script>
</body>

</html>

    <script src="../js/main.js"></script>
    <script>
        if (!checkAdminLogin()) { }
        var admin = getAdminInfo();
        updateHeaderUI();

        // Show staff management if super-admin
        if (admin.role === 'super-admin') {
            document.getElementById('navStaff').style.display = 'block';
            document.getElementById('navDepts').style.display = 'block';
        }

        // Apply restrictions for assigned staff
        applyRestrictions('filterDept', 'filterSem');
        applyRestrictions('dept', 'semester'); // Also for the add modal

        // Form state
        function toggleTypeInputs() {
            var type = document.getElementById('resourceType').value;
            var fileBox = document.getElementById('fileInputContainer');
            var linkBox = document.getElementById('linkInputContainer');
            var textBox = document.getElementById('textInputContainer');

            fileBox.style.display = (['syllabus', 'book', 'material', 'others'].includes(type)) ? 'block' : 'none';
            linkBox.style.display = (type === 'link') ? 'block' : 'none';
            textBox.style.display = (type === 'text') ? 'block' : 'none';
            
            // Toggle required
            document.getElementById('resourceFile').required = (['syllabus', 'book', 'material', 'others'].includes(type));
            document.getElementById('linkUrl').required = (type === 'link');
            document.getElementById('contentText').required = (type === 'text');
        }

        function loadSubjectsForDept() {
            var dept = document.getElementById('dept').value;
            var sem = document.getElementById('semester').value;
            var subjectSelect = document.getElementById('subject');
            
            // For faculty/super-admin, we allow loading subjects without strict filters
            if (admin.role !== 'super-admin' && admin.role !== 'faculty' && (!dept || !sem)) return;

            fetch('../api/get-subjects.php?dept=' + dept + '&semester=' + sem)
                .then(res => res.json())
                .then(data => {
                    var html = '<option value="">General / All Subjects</option>';
                    if (data.success) {
                        data.data.forEach(s => {
                            html += `<option value="${s.subject_id}">${s.subject_code} - ${s.subject_name}</option>`;
                        });
                    }
                    subjectSelect.innerHTML = html;

                    // Faculty Auto-selection & Locking
                    if (admin.role === 'faculty' && data.success && data.data.length > 0) {
                        subjectSelect.value = data.data[0].subject_id;
                        if (data.data.length === 1) {
                            subjectSelect.disabled = true;
                            subjectSelect.style.backgroundColor = '#f1f5f9';
                        }
                    }
                });
        }

        function loadResources() {
            var dept = document.getElementById('filterDept').value;
            var sem = document.getElementById('filterSem').value;
            var type = document.getElementById('filterType').value;

            var url = '../api/get-resources.php?';
            if (dept && dept !== 'all') url += 'dept=' + dept + '&';
            if (sem && sem !== 'all') url += 'semester=' + sem + '&';
            if (type) url += 'type=' + type;
            
            fetch(url)
                .then(res => res.json())
                .then(data => {
                    var tbody = document.getElementById('resourcesList');
                    if (data.success && data.data.length > 0) {
                        var html = '';
                        data.data.forEach(r => {
                            html += `
                                <tr>
                                    <td>
                                        <div style="font-weight: 600; color: var(--dark);">${r.title}</div>
                                        <span class="resource-type-badge type-${r.resource_type}">${r.resource_type}</span>
                                    </td>
                                    <td><span class="badge badge-info">${r.dept_code} - Sem ${r.semester}</span></td>
                                    <td>${r.subject_name || '<span style="color:#94a3b8">General</span>'}</td>
                                    <td>${formatDate(r.created_at)}</td>
                                    <td>
                                        <div style="display:flex; gap: 5px;">
                                            ${r.file_url ? `<a href="../${r.file_url}" target="_blank" class="btn btn-sm btn-outline"><i class="fas fa-eye"></i> View</a>` : ''}
                                            ${r.link_url ? `<a href="${r.link_url}" target="_blank" class="btn btn-sm btn-outline"><i class="fas fa-link"></i> Link</a>` : ''}
                                            <button onclick='editResource(${JSON.stringify(r)})' class="btn btn-sm btn-outline"><i class="fas fa-pen-to-square"></i></button>
                                            <button onclick="deleteResource(${r.resource_id})" class="btn btn-sm btn-danger"><i class="fas fa-trash-can"></i></button>
                                        </div>
                                    </td>
                                </tr>
                            `;
                        });
                        tbody.innerHTML = html;
                    } else {
                        tbody.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="5" style="text-align: center; padding: 40px; color: #64748b;">No resources found</td></tr>';
                    }
                });
        }

        function deleteResource(id) {
            if (!confirm('Are you sure you want to delete this resource?')) return;
            
            var formData = new FormData();
            formData.append('resource_id', id);
            
            fetch('../api/delete-resource.php', {
                method: 'POST',
                body: formData
            })
            .then(res => res.json())
            .then(data => {
                if (data.success) {
                    loadResources();
                } else {
                    alert(data.message);
                }
            });
        }

        function showAddModal() {
            document.getElementById('resourceForm').reset();
            applyRestrictions('dept', 'semester');
            document.getElementById('editId').value = '';
            toggleTypeInputs();
            document.getElementById('resourceModal').style.display = 'flex';
        }

        function editResource(r) {
            document.getElementById('resourceForm').reset();
            document.getElementById('editId').value = r.resource_id;
            document.getElementById('title').value = r.title;
            document.getElementById('description').value = r.description;
            document.getElementById('resourceType').value = r.resource_type;
            document.getElementById('dept').value = r.dept_code;
            document.getElementById('semester').value = r.semester;
            
            // Re-apply restrictions
            applyRestrictions('dept', 'semester');
            
            // Load subjects and then select the right one
            loadSubjectsForDept();
            setTimeout(() => {
                document.getElementById('subject').value = r.subject_id || '';
            }, 500);

            if (r.resource_type === 'link') {
                document.getElementById('linkUrl').value = r.link_url;
            } else if (r.resource_type === 'text') {
                document.getElementById('contentText').value = r.content_text;
            }

            toggleTypeInputs();
            document.getElementById('resourceModal').style.display = 'flex';
        }

        function closeModal() {
            document.getElementById('resourceModal').style.display = 'none';
        }

        // Form Submission
        document.getElementById('resourceForm').addEventListener('submit', function(e) {
            e.preventDefault();
            
            var formData = new FormData();
            formData.append('title', document.getElementById('title').value);
            formData.append('description', document.getElementById('description').value);
            formData.append('resource_type', document.getElementById('resourceType').value);
            formData.append('dept_code', document.getElementById('dept').value);
            formData.append('semester', document.getElementById('semester').value);
            formData.append('subject_id', document.getElementById('subject').value);
            
            var type = document.getElementById('resourceType').value;
            if (['syllabus', 'book', 'material', 'others'].includes(type)) {
                formData.append('file', document.getElementById('resourceFile').files[0]);
            } else if (type === 'link') {
                formData.append('link_url', document.getElementById('linkUrl').value);
            } else {
                formData.append('content_text', document.getElementById('contentText').value);
            }

            var editId = document.getElementById('editId').value;
            if (editId) {
                formData.append('resource_id', editId);
            }

            var url = editId ? '../api/update-resource.php' : '../api/save-resource.php';
            
            fetch(url, {
                method: 'POST',
                body: formData
            })
            .then(res => res.json())
            .then(data => {
                if (data.success) {
                    showAlert('Resource saved successfully!', 'success');
                    closeModal();
                    loadResources();
                } else {
                    alert('Error: ' + data.message);
                }
            });
        });

        // Initialize
        loadResources();
    </script>
</body>

</html>

<!DOCTYPE html>
