FILE: api\upload-class-routine.php
========================================================================================================================
<?php
// =============================================
// UPLOAD CLASS ROUTINE API
// =============================================

include 'config.php';

$title = isset($_POST['title']) ? trim($_POST['title']) : '';
$dept_code = isset($_POST['dept_code']) ? trim($_POST['dept_code']) : '';
$semester = isset($_POST['semester']) ? trim($_POST['semester']) : '';

if (empty($title) || empty($dept_code) || empty($semester)) {
    sendResponse(false, 'Title, department and semester are required');
}

// Handle file upload
if (!isset($_FILES['file']) || $_FILES['file']['error'] !== UPLOAD_ERR_OK) {
    sendResponse(false, 'Please upload a file');
}

$uploadDir = '../uploads/routines/';
if (!file_exists($uploadDir)) {
    mkdir($uploadDir, 0777, true);
}

$fileExt = strtolower(pathinfo($_FILES['file']['name'], PATHINFO_EXTENSION));
$allowed = ['pdf', 'jpg', 'jpeg', 'png'];

if (!in_array($fileExt, $allowed)) {
    sendResponse(false, 'Invalid file type. Allowed: PDF, JPG, PNG');
}

$newFileName = 'routine_' . $dept_code . '_sem' . $semester . '_' . time() . '.' . $fileExt;
$uploadPath = $uploadDir . $newFileName;

if (!move_uploaded_file($_FILES['file']['tmp_name'], $uploadPath)) {
    sendResponse(false, 'Failed to upload file');
}

$file_url = 'uploads/routines/' . $newFileName;

// Insert new routine
$sql = "INSERT INTO class_routines (title, file_url, dept_code, semester) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)";
$stmt = mysqli_prepare($conn, $sql);
mysqli_stmt_bind_param($stmt, "ssss", $title, $file_url, $dept_code, $semester);

if (mysqli_stmt_execute($stmt)) {
    sendResponse(true, 'Routine uploaded', ['routine_id' => mysqli_insert_id($conn)]);
} else {
    sendResponse(false, 'Database error');
}

mysqli_close($conn);
?>

========================================================================================================================

FILE: api\upload-exam-schedule.php
========================================================================================================================
<?php
// =============================================
// UPLOAD EXAM SCHEDULE FILE API
// =============================================

include 'config.php';

$schedule_type = isset($_POST['schedule_type']) ? trim($_POST['schedule_type']) : '';
$dept_code = isset($_POST['dept_code']) ? trim($_POST['dept_code']) : '';
$semester = isset($_POST['semester']) ? intval($_POST['semester']) : 0;

if (empty($schedule_type) || !in_array($schedule_type, ['sessional', 'final'])) {
    sendResponse(false, 'Invalid schedule type');
}

if (empty($dept_code) || $semester <= 0) {
    sendResponse(false, 'Department and Semester are required');
}

// Handle file upload
if (!isset($_FILES['file']) || $_FILES['file']['error'] !== UPLOAD_ERR_OK) {
    sendResponse(false, 'Please upload a file');
}

$uploadDir = '../uploads/exam-schedules/';
if (!file_exists($uploadDir)) {
    mkdir($uploadDir, 0777, true);
}

$fileExt = strtolower(pathinfo($_FILES['file']['name'], PATHINFO_EXTENSION));
$allowed = ['pdf', 'jpg', 'jpeg', 'png'];

if (!in_array($fileExt, $allowed)) {
    sendResponse(false, 'Invalid file type. Allowed: PDF, JPG, PNG');
}

$newFileName = 'schedule_' . $schedule_type . '_' . $dept_code . '_sem' . $semester . '_' . time() . '.' . $fileExt;
$uploadPath = $uploadDir . $newFileName;

if (!move_uploaded_file($_FILES['file']['tmp_name'], $uploadPath)) {
    sendResponse(false, 'Failed to upload file');
}

$file_url = 'uploads/exam-schedules/' . $newFileName;

// Delete old schedule of same type, dept and sem first
$sql = "SELECT file_url FROM exam_schedules WHERE schedule_type = ? AND dept_code = ? AND semester = ?";
$stmt = mysqli_prepare($conn, $sql);
mysqli_stmt_bind_param($stmt, "ssi", $schedule_type, $dept_code, $semester);
mysqli_stmt_execute($stmt);
$result = mysqli_stmt_get_result($stmt);
if ($row = mysqli_fetch_assoc($result)) {
    $oldFile = '../' . $row['file_url'];
    if (file_exists($oldFile))
        unlink($oldFile);
}

// Delete old record
mysqli_query($conn, "DELETE FROM exam_schedules WHERE schedule_type = '$schedule_type' AND dept_code = '$dept_code' AND semester = $semester");

// Insert new
$sql = "INSERT INTO exam_schedules (schedule_type, file_url, dept_code, semester) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)";
$stmt = mysqli_prepare($conn, $sql);
mysqli_stmt_bind_param($stmt, "sssi", $schedule_type, $file_url, $dept_code, $semester);

if (mysqli_stmt_execute($stmt)) {
    sendResponse(true, 'Schedule uploaded', ['schedule_id' => mysqli_insert_id($conn)]);
} else {
    sendResponse(false, 'Database error: ' . mysqli_error($conn));
}

mysqli_close($conn);
?>

========================================================================================================================

-- =============================================
-- CAMPUS PORTAL - DATABASE SETUP
-- ICON COMMERCE COLLEGE (ICC)
-- =============================================

-- Create database
CREATE DATABASE IF NOT EXISTS campus_portal;
USE campus_portal;

-- =============================================
-- TABLE 1: DEPARTMENTS
-- =============================================
CREATE TABLE departments (
    dept_id INT PRIMARY KEY AUTO_INCREMENT,
    dept_code VARCHAR(10) NOT NULL UNIQUE,
    dept_name VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL
);

-- Insert departments
INSERT INTO departments (dept_code, dept_name) VALUES
('BCA', 'Bachelor of Computer Applications'),
('BBA', 'Bachelor of Business Administration'),
('BA', 'Bachelor of Arts'),
('BCOM', 'Bachelor of Commerce');

-- =============================================
-- TABLE 2: SUBJECTS
-- =============================================
CREATE TABLE subjects (
    subject_id INT PRIMARY KEY AUTO_INCREMENT,
    subject_code VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL,
    subject_name VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    dept_code VARCHAR(10) NOT NULL,
    semester INT NOT NULL,
    FOREIGN KEY (dept_code) REFERENCES departments(dept_code)
);

-- BCA Subjects (Semester 1-6)
INSERT INTO subjects (subject_code, subject_name, dept_code, semester) VALUES
-- Semester 1
('BCA101', 'Computer Fundamentals', 'BCA', 1),
('BCA102', 'Mathematics I', 'BCA', 1),

('BCA202', 'Mathematics II', 'BCA', 2),

-- Semester 3

-- =============================================
-- TABLE 3: STUDENTS
-- =============================================
CREATE TABLE students (
    student_id INT PRIMARY KEY AUTO_INCREMENT,
    roll_number VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL UNIQUE,
    name VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    dob DATE NOT NULL,
    email VARCHAR(100),
    phone VARCHAR(15),
    dept_code VARCHAR(10) NOT NULL,
    semester INT NOT NULL,
    created_at TIMESTAMP DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    FOREIGN KEY (dept_code) REFERENCES departments(dept_code)
);

-- =============================================
-- TABLE 4: ADMINS
-- =============================================
CREATE TABLE admins (
    admin_id INT PRIMARY KEY AUTO_INCREMENT,
    username VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL UNIQUE,
    password VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
    name VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    role VARCHAR(50) DEFAULT 'admin'
);

-- Insert default admin (password: admin123)
INSERT INTO admins (username, password, name, role) VALUES
('admin', 'admin123', 'Dr. Sharma', 'super-admin'),
('staff', 'staff123', 'Mr. Verma', 'staff');

-- =============================================
-- TABLE 5: ATTENDANCE
-- =============================================
CREATE TABLE attendance (
    attendance_id INT PRIMARY KEY AUTO_INCREMENT,
    roll_number VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL,
    subject_id INT NOT NULL,
    attendance_date DATE NOT NULL,
    status ENUM('present', 'absent') DEFAULT 'present',
    marked_by VARCHAR(50),
    created_at TIMESTAMP DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    FOREIGN KEY (roll_number) REFERENCES students(roll_number),
    FOREIGN KEY (subject_id) REFERENCES subjects(subject_id),
    UNIQUE KEY unique_attendance (roll_number, subject_id, attendance_date)
);

-- =============================================
-- TABLE 6: EXAMS
-- =============================================
CREATE TABLE exams (
    exam_id INT PRIMARY KEY AUTO_INCREMENT,
    subject_id INT NOT NULL,
    exam_date DATE NOT NULL,
    start_time TIME NOT NULL,
    end_time TIME NOT NULL,
    room VARCHAR(50),
    exam_type ENUM('sessional', 'final') DEFAULT 'final',
    attachment_url VARCHAR(255),
    created_at TIMESTAMP DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    FOREIGN KEY (subject_id) REFERENCES subjects(subject_id)
);

-- Sample exam timetable for BCA Semester 5
INSERT INTO exams (subject_id, exam_date, start_time, end_time, room, exam_type) VALUES
(13, '2026-02-15', '10:00:00', '13:00:00', 'Lab 101', 'sessional'),
(14, '2026-02-17', '10:00:00', '13:00:00', 'Lab 102', 'sessional'),
(15, '2026-02-19', '14:00:00', '17:00:00', 'Room 203', 'sessional'),
(13, '2026-04-10', '10:00:00', '13:00:00', 'Hall A', 'final'),
(14, '2026-04-12', '10:00:00', '13:00:00', 'Hall A', 'final'),
(15, '2026-04-14', '14:00:00', '17:00:00', 'Hall B', 'final');

-- =============================================
-- TABLE 7: LOST AND FOUND
-- =============================================
CREATE TABLE lost_found (
    item_id INT PRIMARY KEY AUTO_INCREMENT,
    title VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    description TEXT,
    category ENUM('id-card', 'phone', 'wallet', 'books', 'electronics', 'other') DEFAULT 'other',
    item_type ENUM('lost', 'found') NOT NULL,
    location VARCHAR(100),
    item_date DATE,
    contact_info VARCHAR(100),
    image_url VARCHAR(255),
    status ENUM('active', 'claimed') DEFAULT 'active',
    posted_by VARCHAR(50),
    claimed_by VARCHAR(20),
    claimed_date DATE,
    created_at TIMESTAMP DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);

-- Sample lost and found items
INSERT INTO lost_found (title, description, category, item_type, location, item_date, contact_info, status, posted_by) VALUES

-- =============================================
-- TABLE 8: ANNOUNCEMENTS
-- =============================================
CREATE TABLE announcements (
    announcement_id INT PRIMARY KEY AUTO_INCREMENT,
    title VARCHAR(200) NOT NULL,
    description TEXT NOT NULL,
    attachment_url VARCHAR(255),
    link_url VARCHAR(255),
    priority ENUM('high', 'medium', 'low') DEFAULT 'medium',
    target_dept VARCHAR(50) DEFAULT 'all',
    target_semester VARCHAR(20) DEFAULT 'all',
    posted_by VARCHAR(50),
    created_at TIMESTAMP DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);

-- Sample announcements
INSERT INTO announcements (title, description, priority, target_dept, target_semester, posted_by) VALUES

('Sports Day Registration', 'Annual sports day is scheduled for February 20, 2026. Register with your department sports coordinator by January 25.', 'medium', 'all', 'all', 'admin');

-- =============================================
-- TABLE 9: CLASS ROUTINES (One file per dept+sem)
-- =============================================
CREATE TABLE class_routines (
    routine_id INT PRIMARY KEY AUTO_INCREMENT,
    title VARCHAR(200) NOT NULL,
    file_url VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
    dept_code VARCHAR(10) NOT NULL,
    semester VARCHAR(10) NOT NULL,
    created_at TIMESTAMP DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);

-- Sample class routine
INSERT INTO class_routines (title, file_url, dept_code, semester) VALUES
('BCA Semester 5 Weekly Routine', 'uploads/routines/bca_sem5_routine.pdf', 'BCA', '5');

-- =============================================
-- TABLE 11: RESOURCES (Syllabus, Books, Materials, Links, Text)
-- =============================================
CREATE TABLE resources (
    resource_id INT PRIMARY KEY AUTO_INCREMENT,
    title VARCHAR(200) NOT NULL,
    description TEXT,
    resource_type ENUM('syllabus', 'book', 'material', 'link', 'text', 'others') NOT NULL,
    file_url VARCHAR(255),
    link_url TEXT,
    content_text TEXT,
    dept_code VARCHAR(10) NOT NULL,
    semester VARCHAR(10) NOT NULL,
    subject_id INT,
    posted_by VARCHAR(50),
    created_at TIMESTAMP DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    FOREIGN KEY (dept_code) REFERENCES departments(dept_code),
    FOREIGN KEY (subject_id) REFERENCES subjects(subject_id)
);

-- =============================================
-- TABLE 10: EXAM SCHEDULES (One file per type)
-- =============================================
CREATE TABLE exam_schedules (
    schedule_id INT PRIMARY KEY AUTO_INCREMENT,
    schedule_type ENUM('sessional', 'final') NOT NULL UNIQUE,
    file_url VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
    created_at TIMESTAMP DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);

-- =============================================
-- HELPFUL VIEWS (Optional but useful)
-- =============================================

-- View: Student attendance summary
CREATE VIEW student_attendance_summary AS
SELECT 
    s.roll_number,
    s.name,
    s.dept_code,
    s.semester,
    sub.subject_name,
    sub.subject_id,
    COUNT(a.attendance_id) as total_classes,
    SUM(CASE WHEN a.status = 'present' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) as present_count,
    ROUND((SUM(CASE WHEN a.status = 'present' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) / COUNT(a.attendance_id)) * 100, 1) as percentage
FROM students s
JOIN subjects sub ON s.dept_code = sub.dept_code AND s.semester = sub.semester
LEFT JOIN attendance a ON s.roll_number = a.roll_number AND sub.subject_id = a.subject_id
GROUP BY s.roll_number, s.name, s.dept_code, s.semester, sub.subject_name, sub.subject_id;

-- =============================================
CREATE TABLE departments (
    dept_id INT PRIMARY KEY AUTO_INCREMENT,
    dept_code VARCHAR(10) NOT NULL UNIQUE,
    dept_name VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL
);

-- =============================================
-- TABLE 4: ADMINS
-- =============================================
CREATE TABLE admins (
    admin_id INT PRIMARY KEY AUTO_INCREMENT,
    username VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL UNIQUE,
    password VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
    name VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    role VARCHAR(50) DEFAULT 'admin'
);

-- =============================================
-- TABLE 5: ATTENDANCE
-- =============================================
CREATE TABLE attendance (
    attendance_id INT PRIMARY KEY AUTO_INCREMENT,
    roll_number VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL,
    subject_id INT NOT NULL,
    attendance_date DATE NOT NULL,
    status ENUM('present', 'absent') DEFAULT 'present',
    marked_by VARCHAR(50),
    created_at TIMESTAMP DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    FOREIGN KEY (roll_number) REFERENCES students(roll_number),
    FOREIGN KEY (subject_id) REFERENCES subjects(subject_id),
    UNIQUE KEY unique_attendance (roll_number, subject_id, attendance_date)
);

-- =============================================
-- TABLE 6: EXAMS
-- =============================================
CREATE TABLE exams (
    exam_id INT PRIMARY KEY AUTO_INCREMENT,
    subject_id INT NOT NULL,
    exam_date DATE NOT NULL,
    start_time TIME NOT NULL,
    end_time TIME NOT NULL,
    room VARCHAR(50),
    exam_type ENUM('sessional', 'final') DEFAULT 'final',
    attachment_url VARCHAR(255),
    created_at TIMESTAMP DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    FOREIGN KEY (subject_id) REFERENCES subjects(subject_id)
);

    title VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    description TEXT,
    category ENUM('id-card', 'phone', 'wallet', 'books', 'electronics', 'other') DEFAULT 'other',
    item_type ENUM('lost', 'found') NOT NULL,
    location VARCHAR(100),
    item_date DATE,
    contact_info VARCHAR(100),
    image_url VARCHAR(255),
    status ENUM('active', 'claimed') DEFAULT 'active',
    posted_by VARCHAR(50),
    claimed_by VARCHAR(20),
    claimed_date DATE,
    created_at TIMESTAMP DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);

FILE: diagrams\01_system_architecture.mmd
========================================================================================================================
%% System Architecture — 3-Tier Design
%% ICC Companion College Management System

graph TB
    subgraph CLIENT["TIER 1: CLIENT (Presentation Layer)"]
        direction LR
        A1["Web Browser"]
        A2["HTML5 / CSS3"]
        A3["JavaScript (ES6)"]
        A4["Mobile-Responsive"]
    end

    subgraph SERVER["TIER 2: SERVER (Application Layer)"]
        direction LR
        B1["PHP Backend (XAMPP Apache)"]
        B2["Authentication & Session Management"]
        B3["Business Logic: Attendance, Exams, Resources, Routines"]
        B4["Role-Based Access Control"]
    end

    subgraph DATABASE["TIER 3: DATABASE (Data Layer)"]
        direction LR
        C1["MySQL (phpMyAdmin)"]
        C2["11 Tables + 1 View"]
        C3["Normalized Schema with Foreign Keys"]
    end

    CLIENT -->|"HTTP Requests / AJAX"| SERVER
    SERVER -->|"MySQL Queries (PDO)"| DATABASE
    DATABASE -->|"Result Sets"| SERVER
    SERVER -->|"HTML / JSON Responses"| CLIENT

    style CLIENT fill:#e8f0fe,stroke:#1a73e8,stroke-width:2px
    style SERVER fill:#fef7e0,stroke:#f9a825,stroke-width:2px
    style DATABASE fill:#e6f4ea,stroke:#34a853,stroke-width:2px

    style A1 fill:#e8f0fe,stroke:none
    style A2 fill:#e8f0fe,stroke:none
    style A3 fill:#e8f0fe,stroke:none
    style A4 fill:#e8f0fe,stroke:none

    style B1 fill:#fef7e0,stroke:none
    style B2 fill:#fef7e0,stroke:none
    style B3 fill:#fef7e0,stroke:none
    style B4 fill:#fef7e0,stroke:none

    style C1 fill:#e6f4ea,stroke:none
    style C2 fill:#e6f4ea,stroke:none
    style C3 fill:#e6f4ea,stroke:none

FILE: diagrams\02_dfd_level0.mmd

%% DFD Level 0 — Context Diagram
%% ICC Companion College Management System

graph LR
    STUDENT["`**Student**
    End User`"]
    FACULTY["`**Faculty**
    Subject Teacher`"]
    ADMIN["`**Admin**
    HOD / Principal`"]
    SYSTEM("`**ICC Companion**
    College Management System`")
    DB[("`**Database**
    MySQL`")]

    STUDENT -->|"Login Credentials (Roll + DOB)"| SYSTEM
    SYSTEM -->|"Attendance %, Resources, Routines"| STUDENT

    FACULTY -->|"Attendance Data, Resource Uploads"| SYSTEM
    SYSTEM -->|"Subject Lists, Student Rosters"| FACULTY

    ADMIN -->|"CRUD Commands, Configuration"| SYSTEM
    SYSTEM -->|"Dashboard Data, Reports"| ADMIN

    SYSTEM -->|"SQL Queries"| DB
    DB -->|"Query Results"| SYSTEM

    style STUDENT fill:#fef7e0,stroke:#e37400,stroke-width:2px
    style FACULTY fill:#fef7e0,stroke:#e37400,stroke-width:2px
    style ADMIN fill:#e6f4ea,stroke:#137333,stroke-width:2px
    style DB fill:#e6f4ea,stroke:#137333,stroke-width:2px,stroke-dasharray: 5 5

    style SYSTEM fill:#e8f0fe,stroke:#1a73e8,stroke-width:3px

    linkStyle default stroke:#5f6368,stroke-width:2px

%% DFD Level 1 — Process Breakdown
%% ICC Companion College Management System

graph TB
    STUDENT["`**Student**`"]
    FACULTY["`**Faculty**`"]
    ADMIN["`**Admin**`"]

    subgraph SYSTEM["ICC Companion — System Processes"]
        P1("`**P1**
        Authentication
        & Login`")
        P2("`**P2**
        Attendance
        Management`")
        P3("`**P3**
        Resource
        Library`")
        P4("`**P4**
        Exam &
        Routine View`")
        P5("`**P5**
        Lost & Found
        Management`")
        P6("`**P6**
        Announcement
        Management`")
    end

    D1[("Students DB")]
    D2[("Attendance DB")]
    D3[("Resources DB")]
    D4[("Exams DB")]
    D5[("Lost & Found DB")]
    D6[("Announcements DB")]

    STUDENT -->|"Login Request"| P1
    P1 -->|"Session Token"| STUDENT

    STUDENT -->|"View Request"| P4
    P4 -->|"Routine Data"| STUDENT

    STUDENT -->|"Download Request"| P3
    P3 -->|"File Stream"| STUDENT

    STUDENT -->|"Report Item"| P5
    P5 -->|"Claim Status"| STUDENT

    STUDENT -->|"Browse"| P6
    P6 -->|"Notices"| STUDENT

    FACULTY -->|"Attendance Entry"| P2
    P2 -->|"Student Roster"| FACULTY
    FACULTY -->|"Upload File"| P3

    ADMIN -->|"Manage All"| P6
    ADMIN -->|"CRUD"| P2
    ADMIN -->|"CRUD"| P4
    ADMIN -->|"Oversee"| P5

    P1 --> D1
    P2 --> D2
    P3 --> D3
    P4 --> D4
    P5 --> D5
    P6 --> D6

    D1 -.-> P2
    D2 -.-> P4

    style STUDENT fill:#fef7e0,stroke:#e37400,stroke-width:2px
    style FACULTY fill:#fef7e0,stroke:#e37400,stroke-width:2px
    style ADMIN fill:#e6f4ea,stroke:#137333,stroke-width:2px

    style P1 fill:#e8f0fe,stroke:#1a73e8,stroke-width:2px
    style P2 fill:#e8f0fe,stroke:#1a73e8,stroke-width:2px
    style P3 fill:#e8f0fe,stroke:#1a73e8,stroke-width:2px
    style P4 fill:#e8f0fe,stroke:#1a73e8,stroke-width:2px
    style P5 fill:#e8f0fe,stroke:#1a73e8,stroke-width:2px
    style P6 fill:#e8f0fe,stroke:#1a73e8,stroke-width:2px

    style D1 fill:#e6f4ea,stroke:#34a853,stroke-width:2px,stroke-dasharray:5 5
    style D2 fill:#e6f4ea,stroke:#34a853,stroke-width:2px,stroke-dasharray:5 5
    style D3 fill:#e6f4ea,stroke:#34a853,stroke-width:2px,stroke-dasharray:5 5
    style D4 fill:#e6f4ea,stroke:#34a853,stroke-width:2px,stroke-dasharray:5 5
    style D5 fill:#e6f4ea,stroke:#34a853,stroke-width:2px,stroke-dasharray:5 5

---
title: ER Diagram — ICC Companion Database
---

erDiagram
    FACULTY_SUBJECTS {
        int id PK
        int admin_id FK
        int subject_id FK
        varchar dept_code
        int semester
    }

    DEPARTMENTS ||--o{ STUDENTS : "1 : N"
    DEPARTMENTS ||--o{ ADMINS : "1 : N"
    DEPARTMENTS ||--o{ SUBJECTS : "1 : N"

    SUBJECTS ||--o{ ATTENDANCE : "1 : N"
    SUBJECTS ||--o{ EXAMS : "1 : N"
    SUBJECTS ||--o{ CLASS_ROUTINES : "1 : N"
    SUBJECTS ||--o{ RESOURCES : "1 : N"

    STUDENTS ||--o{ ATTENDANCE : "1 : N"
    STUDENTS ||--o{ LOST_FOUND : "1 : N"

    ADMINS ||--o{ ATTENDANCE : "1 : N"
    ADMINS ||--o{ CLASS_ROUTINES : "1 : N"
    ADMINS ||--o{ RESOURCES : "1 : N"
    ADMINS ||--o{ ANNOUNCEMENTS : "1 : N"
    ADMINS ||--o{ LOST_FOUND : "1 : N"
    ADMINS ||--o{ EXAM_SCHEDULES : "1 : N"

    FACULTY_SUBJECTS ||--o{ ATTENDANCE : "1 : N"
    SUBJECTS ||--o{ FACULTY_SUBJECTS : "1 : N"
    ADMINS ||--o{ FACULTY_SUBJECTS : "1 : N"

    DEPARTMENTS {
        int dept_id PK
        varchar dept_code
        varchar dept_name
    }

    SUBJECTS {
        int subject_id PK
        varchar subject_name
        varchar subject_code
        int dept_id FK
        int semester
    }

    STUDENTS {
        varchar roll_no PK
        varchar name
        date dob
        varchar email
        varchar phone
        int dept_id FK
        int semester
    }

    ADMINS {
        int admin_id PK
        varchar username
        varchar password
        varchar name
        enum role
        int dept_id FK
    }

    ATTENDANCE {
        int attendance_id PK
        varchar student_id FK
        int subject_id FK
        date date
        enum status
        int marked_by FK
    }

    EXAMS {
        int exam_id PK
        int subject_id FK
        date exam_date
        int total_marks
        int pass_marks
    }

    ANNOUNCEMENTS {
        int announcement_id PK
        varchar title
        text description
        enum priority
        varchar file
        int created_by FK
    }

    CLASS_ROUTINES {
        int routine_id PK
        int subject_id FK
        varchar day_of_week
        time time_slot
        varchar room_no
        int faculty_id FK
    }

    RESOURCES {
        int resource_id PK
        int subject_id FK
        varchar title
        varchar file_path
        enum type
        int uploaded_by FK
    }

    LOST_FOUND {
        int item_id PK
        varchar title
        varchar category
        enum status
        varchar reported_by FK
        varchar image
        enum claim_status
    }

    EXAM_SCHEDULES {
        int schedule_id PK
        int exam_id FK
        int subject_id FK
        date exam_date
        time start_time
        time end_time
        int created_by FK
    }

FILE: js\main.js
========================================================================================================================
// =============================================
// ICC COMPANION - MAIN JAVASCRIPT
// ICON COMMERCE COLLEGE (ICC)
// =============================================

// Base URL for API calls
var API_BASE = 'api/';

// =============================================
// SESSION MANAGEMENT
// =============================================

// Check if student is logged in
function checkStudentLogin() {
    var roll = sessionStorage.getItem('studentRoll');
    if (!roll) {
        window.location.href = '../student-login.html';
        return false;
    }
    return true;
}

// Check if admin is logged in
function checkAdminLogin() {
    var adminId = sessionStorage.getItem('adminId');
    if (!adminId) {
        window.location.href = '../admin-login.html';
        return false;
    }
    return true;
}

// Logout function
function logout(type) {
    sessionStorage.clear();
    if (type === 'student') {
        window.location.href = '../student-login.html';
    } else {
        window.location.href = '../admin-login.html';
    }
}

// Get student info from session
function getStudentInfo() {
    return {
        roll: sessionStorage.getItem('studentRoll'),
        name: sessionStorage.getItem('studentName'),
        dept: sessionStorage.getItem('studentDept'),
        semester: sessionStorage.getItem('studentSemester')
    };
}

// Get admin info from session
function getAdminInfo() {
    return {
        id: sessionStorage.getItem('adminId'),
        name: sessionStorage.getItem('adminName'),
        role: sessionStorage.getItem('adminRole'),
        assignedDept: sessionStorage.getItem('assignedDept') || 'all',
        assignedSemester: sessionStorage.getItem('assignedSemester') || 'all'
    };
}

// =============================================
// API HELPER FUNCTIONS
// =============================================

// Make GET request
function apiGet(endpoint, callback) {
    fetch(API_BASE + endpoint)
        .then(function (response) {
            return response.json();
        })
        .then(function (data) {
            callback(null, data);
        })
        .catch(function (error) {
            callback(error, null);
        });
}

// Make POST request
function apiPost(endpoint, formData, callback) {
    fetch(API_BASE + endpoint, {
        method: 'POST',
        body: formData
    })
        .then(function (response) {
            return response.json();
        })
        .then(function (data) {
            callback(null, data);
        })
        .catch(function (error) {
            callback(error, null);
        });
}

// =============================================
// UI HELPER FUNCTIONS
// =============================================

// Show loading indicator
function showLoading(elementId) {
    var element = document.getElementById(elementId);
    if (element) {
        element.innerHTML = '<div style="text-align: center; padding: 40px; color: #64748b;">Loading...</div>';
    }
}

// Show alert message
function showAlert(message, type) {
    var alertDiv = document.createElement('div');
    alertDiv.className = 'alert alert-' + type;
    alertDiv.textContent = message;

    var container = document.querySelector('.container');
    if (container) {
        container.insertBefore(alertDiv, container.firstChild);

        // Auto remove after 5 seconds
        setTimeout(function () {
            alertDiv.remove();
        }, 5000);
    }
}

// Format date for display
function formatDate(dateString) {
    var date = new Date(dateString);
    var options = { year: 'numeric', month: 'short', day: 'numeric' };
    return date.toLocaleDateString('en-IN', options);
}

// Calculate days remaining
function daysRemaining(dateString) {
    var today = new Date();
    today.setHours(0, 0, 0, 0);
    var target = new Date(dateString);
    var diff = target - today;
    var days = Math.ceil(diff / (1000 * 60 * 60 * 24));
    return days;
}

// Get attendance status class
function getAttendanceStatus(percentage) {
    if (percentage >= 75) {
        return { class: 'green', text: 'Safe', badge: 'badge-success' };
    } else if (percentage >= 65) {
        return { class: 'orange', text: 'Warning', badge: 'badge-warning' };
    } else {
        return { class: 'red', text: 'Shortage', badge: 'badge-danger' };
    }
}

// Calculate classes that can be missed
function classesCanMiss(present, total, required) {
    required = required || 75;
    // Formula: present / (total + x) >= required/100
    // x = (present * 100 / required) - total
    var maxTotal = Math.floor((present * 100) / required);
    var canMiss = maxTotal - total;
    return canMiss > 0 ? canMiss : 0;
}

// =============================================
// RESTRICTION ENFORCEMENT
// =============================================

// Lock filters for assigned staff
function applyRestrictions(deptSelectorId, semSelectorId) {
    var admin = getAdminInfo();
    if (admin.role === 'super-admin') return; 

    // Faculty (Subject-wise) scoping: 
    // Usually they teach across Depts, so we default their filters to 'all' to show all assigned subjects
    if (admin.role === 'faculty') {
        var dEl = document.getElementById(deptSelectorId);
        var sEl = document.getElementById(semSelectorId);
        if (dEl) {
            dEl.value = admin.assignedDept || 'all';
            dEl.disabled = true;
            dEl.style.backgroundColor = '#f8fafc';
            dEl.style.color = '#64748b';
            dEl.style.borderColor = '#e2e8f0';
            dEl.style.cursor = 'not-allowed';
            dEl.style.opacity = '0.8';
            var event = new Event('change');
            dEl.dispatchEvent(event);
        }
        if (sEl) {
            sEl.value = admin.assignedSemester || 'all';
            sEl.disabled = true;
            sEl.style.backgroundColor = '#f8fafc';
            sEl.style.color = '#64748b';
            sEl.style.borderColor = '#e2e8f0';
            sEl.style.cursor = 'not-allowed';
            sEl.style.opacity = '0.8';
            var event = new Event('change');
            sEl.dispatchEvent(event);
        }
        return;
    }

    if (admin.assignedDept !== 'all') {
        var deptEl = document.getElementById(deptSelectorId);
        if (deptEl) {
            deptEl.value = admin.assignedDept;
            deptEl.disabled = true;
            deptEl.style.backgroundColor = '#f8fafc';
            deptEl.style.color = '#64748b';
            deptEl.style.borderColor = '#e2e8f0';
            deptEl.style.cursor = 'not-allowed';
            deptEl.style.opacity = '0.8';
            
            // Trigger change event to load data
            var event = new Event('change');
            deptEl.dispatchEvent(event);
        }
    }

    if (admin.assignedSemester !== 'all') {
        var semEl = document.getElementById(semSelectorId);
        if (semEl) {
            semEl.value = admin.assignedSemester;
            semEl.disabled = true;
            semEl.style.backgroundColor = '#f8fafc';
            semEl.style.color = '#64748b';
            semEl.style.borderColor = '#e2e8f0';
            semEl.style.cursor = 'not-allowed';
            semEl.style.opacity = '0.8';
            
            // Trigger change event to load data
            var event = new Event('change');
            semEl.dispatchEvent(event);
        }
    }
}

// Mark active navigation link
function setActiveNav(page) {
    var links = document.querySelectorAll('.nav-menu a');
    links.forEach(function (link) {
        link.classList.remove('active');
        if (link.getAttribute('href').includes(page)) {
            link.classList.add('active');
        }
    });
}

// Enforce role-based menu permissions
function enforceSidebarPermissions() {
    var admin = getAdminInfo();
    if (!admin.id) return;

    // Items that are ONLY for Super Admins
    var superOnly = ['staff.html', 'departments.html'];
    
    // Items that are HIDDEN for Faculty (Subject Teachers)
    var hodOnly = ['subjects.html', 'exams.html', 'routines.html', 'announcements.html', 'lost-found.html'];

    var navLinks = document.querySelectorAll('.nav-menu a');
    navLinks.forEach(function(link) {
        var href = link.getAttribute('href');
        var li = link.parentElement;

        // Hide Principal-only items (Staff and Departments)
        if (admin.role !== 'super-admin' && (href.includes('staff.html') || href.includes('departments.html'))) {
            li.style.display = 'none';
        }

        // Hide HOD/Principal items for Faculty
        if (admin.role === 'faculty' && hodOnly.some(function(item) { return href.includes(item); })) {
            li.style.display = 'none';
        }
    });
}

// Set user info in header with role and scope
function updateHeaderUI() {
    var admin = getAdminInfo();
    var nameEl = document.getElementById('adminName');
    if (nameEl) {
        var roleText = 'Faculty';
        if (admin.role === 'super-admin') roleText = 'Principal';
        else if (admin.role === 'dept-admin') roleText = 'HOD';

        var scopeText = '';
        if (admin.role !== 'super-admin') {
            scopeText = ' (' + (admin.assignedDept === 'all' ? 'All Depts' : admin.assignedDept);
            if (admin.role === 'faculty') {
                scopeText += ' - ' + (admin.assignedSemester === 'all' ? 'All Sem' : 'Sem ' + admin.assignedSemester);
            }
            scopeText += ')';
        }
        nameEl.innerHTML = admin.name + ' <small style="color: #94a3b8; font-weight: normal; margin-left: 5px;">[' + roleText + scopeText + ']</small>';
    }
    
    // Also enforce sidebar permissions whenever header is updated
    enforceSidebarPermissions();
}

// Populate department dropdowns dynamically
function populateDepartments(selectorId, callback) {
    var selector = document.getElementById(selectorId);
    if (!selector) return;

    fetch('../api/get-departments.php')
        .then(function(res) { return res.json(); })
        .then(function(data) {
            if (data.success) {
                var html = selector.getAttribute('data-allow-all') === 'true' ? '<option value="all">All Departments</option>' : '<option value="">Select Dept</option>';
                data.data.forEach(function(dept) {
                    html += '<option value="' + dept.dept_code + '" data-max-sem="' + dept.max_semesters + '">' + dept.dept_name + ' (' + dept.dept_code + ')</option>';
                });
                selector.innerHTML = html;
                
                // CRITICAL: Re-apply value if it was set before population
                var admin = getAdminInfo();
                if (admin.role !== 'super-admin') {
                    // For Faculty/HOD, the value might be locked to 'all' or their assigned dept
                    if (admin.role === 'faculty' || admin.assignedDept !== 'all') {
                        selector.value = admin.assignedDept || 'all';
                        // Trigger change event to load semesters/subjects
                        var event = new Event('change');
                        selector.dispatchEvent(event);
                    }
                }

                if (callback) callback(data.data);
            }
        });
}

// Update semester dropdown based on selected department's max semesters
function updateSemesterDropdown(deptSelectorId, semSelectorId) {
    var deptEl = document.getElementById(deptSelectorId);
    var semEl = document.getElementById(semSelectorId);
    if (!deptEl || !semEl) return;

    var selectedOption = deptEl.options[deptEl.selectedIndex];
    var maxSem = selectedOption ? parseInt(selectedOption.getAttribute('data-max-sem')) : 6;
    if (deptEl.value === 'all' || !deptEl.value) maxSem = 8; // Default fallback

    var html = semEl.getAttribute('data-allow-all') === 'true' ? '<option value="all">All Semesters</option>' : '<option value="">Select Sem</option>';
    for (var i = 1; i <= maxSem; i++) {
        html += '<option value="' + i + '">' + i + '</option>';
    }
    semEl.innerHTML = html;

    // CRITICAL: Re-apply restricted value for Faculty/HOD
    var admin = getAdminInfo();
    if (admin.role !== 'super-admin') {
        if (admin.role === 'faculty' || admin.assignedSemester !== 'all') {
            semEl.value = admin.assignedSemester || 'all';
            // Trigger change if needed (e.g. to load subjects)
            var event = new Event('change');
            semEl.dispatchEvent(event);
        }
    }
}

# ICC Companion - ICON COMMERCE COLLEGE
## Setup Guide for XAMPP

---

## 📋 What You Need

1. **XAMPP** - Download from: https://www.apachefriends.org/
2. **Web Browser** - Chrome, Firefox, or Edge

---

## 🚀 Step-by-Step Setup Instructions

### Step 1: Install XAMPP

1. Download XAMPP from the official website
2. Run the installer
3. Install with default settings (Apache + MySQL + PHP)
4. Installation folder: `C:\xampp`

---

### Step 2: Copy Project Files

1. Copy the entire `ICC_Companion` folder
2. Paste it inside: `C:\xampp\htdocs\`
3. Your folder structure should be:
   ```
   C:\xampp\htdocs\ICC_Companion\
   ├── index.html
   ├── student-login.html
   ├── admin-login.html
   ├── css\
   ├── js\
   ├── api\
   ├── student\
   ├── admin\
   └── database\
   ```

---

### Step 3: Start XAMPP

1. Open **XAMPP Control Panel**
2. Click **Start** next to **Apache** (for web server)
3. Click **Start** next to **MySQL** (for database)
4. Both should show "Running" in green

---

### Step 4: Create Database

1. Open your browser
2. Go to: `http://localhost/phpmyadmin`
3. Click on **"Import"** tab at the top
4. Click **"Choose File"** button
5. Select: `ICC_Companion/database/campus_portal.sql`
6. Click **"Go"** button at the bottom

**If successful, you'll see:**
- A new database called `campus_portal`
- Tables like: students, admins, attendance, exams, etc.

---

### Step 5: Test the Application

1. Open browser
2. Go to: `http://localhost/ICC_Companion`
3. You should see the ICC Companion home page!

---

## 🎯 Features Overview

### Student Side (View Only)
- ✅ Login with Roll Number + DOB
- ✅ View attendance with percentages
- ✅ View exam timetable
- ✅ Browse lost & found items
- ✅ Read announcements

### Admin Side (Full Control)
- ✅ Add/Edit/Delete students
- ✅ Mark attendance (easy checkbox system)
- ✅ Manage exam schedule
- ✅ Post/Manage lost & found items
- ✅ Post/Delete announcements

---

## ❓ Troubleshooting

### "Database connection failed"
- Make sure MySQL is running in XAMPP
- Check if database `campus_portal` exists in phpMyAdmin

### "404 Page Not Found"
- Check if files are in `C:\xampp\htdocs\campus-portal\`
- Make sure Apache is running

### "Login not working"
- Check roll number format (e.g., BCA23001)
- Date format must be YYYY-MM-DD

---

## 📝 Technologies Used

- **Frontend**: HTML, CSS, JavaScript
- **Backend**: PHP
- **Database**: MySQL
- **Server**: Apache (via XAMPP)

---

## 🎓 Project By

**ICON COMMERCE COLLEGE**  
BCA Final Year Project - ICC Companion

---

*For any issues, re-import the database SQL file and restart XAMPP.*

<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="en">

<head>
    <meta charset="UTF-8">
    <meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0">
    <title>Announcements - ICC Companion</title>
    <link rel="stylesheet" href="../css/style.css">
</head>

<body>
    <!-- Header -->
    <div class="header">
        <div class="logo">
            <span class="logo-icon"><i class="fas fa-graduation-cap"></i></span>
            <div>
                <div class="college-name">ICC Companion</div>
                <div class="college-short">ICON COMMERCE COLLEGE</div>
            </div>
        </div>
        <div class="user-info">
            <span class="user-name">Welcome, <strong id="userName">Student</strong></span>
            <button class="btn-logout" onclick="logout('student')">Logout</button>
        </div>
    </div>

    <!-- Navigation -->
    <nav class="nav-menu">
        <ul>
            <li><a href="dashboard.html"><i class="fas fa-house"></i> Dashboard</a></li>
            <li><a href="subjects.html"><i class="fas fa-book-open"></i> Subjects</a></li>
            <li><a href="resources.html"><i class="fas fa-book"></i> Resources</a></li>
            <li><a href="attendance.html"><i class="fas fa-chart-bar"></i> Attendance</a></li>
            <li><a href="exams.html"><i class="fas fa-calendar"></i> Exams</a></li>
            <li><a href="routines.html"><i class="fas fa-clipboard"></i> Routines</a></li>
            <li><a href="lost-found.html"><i class="fas fa-magnifying-glass"></i> Lost & Found</a></li>
            <li><a href="announcements.html" class="active"><i class="fas fa-bullhorn"></i> Announcements</a></li>
        </ul>
    </nav>

    <!-- Main Content -->
    <div class="container">
        <h1 class="page-title"><i class="fas fa-bullhorn"></i> Announcements</h1>

        <!-- Announcements List -->
        <div id="announcementsList">
            <div style="text-align: center; padding: 40px; color: #64748b;">Loading announcements...</div>
        </div>
    </div>

    <script src="../js/main.js"></script>
    <script>
        // Check if logged in
        if (!checkStudentLogin()) {
            // Will redirect to login
        }

        // Get student info and display
        var student = getStudentInfo();
        document.getElementById('userName').textContent = student.name;

        // Load announcements
        loadAnnouncements();

        function loadAnnouncements() {
            fetch('../api/get-announcements.php')
                .then(function (res) { return res.json(); })
                .then(function (data) {
                    var container = document.getElementById('announcementsList');

                    if (data.success && data.data.length > 0) {
                        var html = '';

                        data.data.forEach(function (item) {
                            var priorityClass = item.priority === 'high' ? 'high' : '';
                            var priorityBadge = '';
                            if (item.priority === 'high') {
                                priorityBadge = '<span class="badge badge-danger">Urgent</span> ';
                            } else if (item.priority === 'medium') {
                                priorityBadge = '<span class="badge badge-warning">Important</span> ';
                            }

                            html += '<div class="card announcement-item ' + priorityClass + '" style="border-left: 4px solid ' + (item.priority === 'high' ? 'var(--danger)' : 'var(--primary)') + ';">';
                            html += '  <div style="display: flex; justify-content: space-between; align-items: flex-start; margin-bottom: 10px;">';
                            html += '    <div class="title" style="font-size: 16px;">' + priorityBadge + item.title + '</div>';
                            html += '    <div class="date" style="font-size: 12px; color: #64748b; white-space: nowrap;">' + formatDate(item.created_at) + '</div>';
                            html += '  </div>';
                            html += '  <div style="font-size: 14px; color: #475569; line-height: 1.6;">' + item.description + '</div>';
                            if (item.target_dept !== 'all' || item.target_semester !== 'all') {
                                html += '  <div style="margin-top: 10px; font-size: 12px; color: #64748b;">';
                                html += '    For: ';
                                if (item.target_dept !== 'all') html += item.target_dept + ' ';
                                if (item.target_semester !== 'all') html += 'Semester ' + item.target_semester;
                                html += '  </div>';
                            }
                            if (item.attachment_url || item.link_url) {
                                html += '  <div style="margin-top: 12px; display: flex; gap: 10px; flex-wrap: wrap;">';
                                if (item.attachment_url) {
                                    html += '    <a href="../' + item.attachment_url + '" target="_blank" class="btn btn-sm btn-outline"><i class="fas fa-paperclip"></i> View Attachment</a>';
                                }
                                if (item.link_url) {
                                    var displayUrl = item.link_url.replace('https://', '').replace('http://', '');
                                    if (displayUrl.length > 40) displayUrl = displayUrl.substring(0, 37) + '...';
                                    html += '    <a href="' + item.link_url + '" target="_blank" class="btn btn-sm btn-outline"><i class="fas fa-link"></i> ' + displayUrl + '</a>';
                                }
                                html += '  </div>';
                            }
                            html += '</div>';
                        });

                        container.innerHTML = html;
                    } else {
                        container.innerHTML = '<div class="card" style="text-align: center; color: #64748b;">No announcements at this time.</div>';
                    }
                })
                .catch(function (error) {
                    console.error('Error:', error);
                    document.getElementById('announcementsList').innerHTML = '<div class="alert alert-error">Failed to load announcements</div>';
                });
        }
    </script>
</body>

</html>

FILE: student\attendance.html

<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="en">

<head>
    <meta charset="UTF-8">
    <meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0">
    <title>My Attendance - ICC Companion</title>
    <link rel="stylesheet" href="../css/style.css">
</head>

<body>
    <!-- Header -->
    <div class="header">
        <div class="logo">
            <span class="logo-icon"><i class="fas fa-graduation-cap"></i></span>
            <div>
                <div class="college-name">ICC Companion</div>
                <div class="college-short">ICON COMMERCE COLLEGE</div>
            </div>
        </div>
        <div class="user-info">
            <span class="user-name">Welcome, <strong id="userName">Student</strong></span>
            <button class="btn-logout" onclick="logout('student')">Logout</button>
        </div>
    </div>

    <!-- Navigation -->
    <nav class="nav-menu">
        <ul>
            <li><a href="dashboard.html"><i class="fas fa-house"></i> Dashboard</a></li>
            <li><a href="subjects.html"><i class="fas fa-book-open"></i> Subjects</a></li>
            <li><a href="resources.html"><i class="fas fa-book"></i> Resources</a></li>
            <li><a href="attendance.html" class="active"><i class="fas fa-chart-bar"></i> Attendance</a></li>
            <li><a href="exams.html"><i class="fas fa-calendar"></i> Exams</a></li>
            <li><a href="routines.html"><i class="fas fa-clipboard"></i> Routines</a></li>
            <li><a href="lost-found.html"><i class="fas fa-magnifying-glass"></i> Lost & Found</a></li>
            <li><a href="announcements.html"><i class="fas fa-bullhorn"></i> Announcements</a></li>
        </ul>
    </nav>

    <!-- Main Content -->
    <div class="container">
        <h1 class="page-title"><i class="fas fa-chart-bar"></i> My Attendance</h1>

        <!-- Overall Summary Card -->
        <div class="card">
            <div
                style="display: flex; justify-content: space-between; align-items: center; flex-wrap: wrap; gap: 15px;">
                <div>
                    <h3 style="margin-bottom: 5px;">Semester <span id="semesterNum">-</span> | <span
                            id="deptName">-</span></h3>
                    <p style="color: #64748b; font-size: 14px;">Minimum required: 75%</p>
                </div>
                <div style="text-align: right;">
                    <div style="font-size: 28px; font-weight: bold; color: var(--primary);" id="overallPercent">--%
                    </div>
                    <div style="font-size: 13px; color: #64748b;">Overall Attendance</div>
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>

        <!-- Subject-wise Attendance -->
        <div id="attendanceList">
            <div style="text-align: center; padding: 40px; color: #64748b;">Loading attendance...</div>
        </div>
    </div>

    <script src="../js/main.js"></script>
    <script>
        // Check if logged in
        if (!checkStudentLogin()) {
            // Will redirect to login
        }

        // Get student info and display
        var student = getStudentInfo();
        document.getElementById('userName').textContent = student.name;
        document.getElementById('semesterNum').textContent = student.semester;
        document.getElementById('deptName').textContent = student.dept;

        // Load attendance
        loadAttendance();

        function loadAttendance() {
            fetch('../api/get-attendance.php?roll=' + student.roll)
                .then(function (res) { return res.json(); })
                .then(function (data) {
                    var container = document.getElementById('attendanceList');

                    if (data.success && data.data.length > 0) {
                        var html = '';
                        var totalPresent = 0;
                        var totalClasses = 0;

                        data.data.forEach(function (subject) {
                            var present = parseInt(subject.present_count);
                            var total = parseInt(subject.total_classes);
                            var percent = parseFloat(subject.percentage);
                            var status = getAttendanceStatus(percent);
                            var canMiss = classesCanMiss(present, total, 75);

                            totalPresent += present;
                            totalClasses += total;

                            html += '<div class="attendance-card">';
                            html += '  <div class="subject-name">' + subject.subject_name + '</div>';
                            html += '  <div class="stats">';
                            html += '    <span>Attended: ' + present + ' / ' + total + ' classes</span>';
                            html += '  </div>';
                            html += '  <div class="progress-bar">';
                            html += '    <div class="fill ' + status.class + '" style="width: ' + percent + '%"></div>';
                            html += '  </div>';
                            html += '  <div class="status">';
                            html += '    <span><span class="badge ' + status.badge + '">' + status.text + '</span> ' + percent + '%</span>';
                            if (status.text === 'Safe' && canMiss > 0) {
                                html += '    <span style="color: var(--secondary);">Can miss ' + canMiss + ' more</span>';
                            } else if (status.text !== 'Safe') {
                                html += '    <span style="color: var(--danger);">Attend more classes!</span>';
                            }
                            html += '  </div>';
                            html += '</div>';
                        });

                        container.innerHTML = html;

                        // Update overall
                        var overallPercent = totalClasses > 0 ? Math.round((totalPresent / totalClasses) * 100) : 0;
                        document.getElementById('overallPercent').textContent = overallPercent + '%';

                    } else {
                        container.innerHTML = '<div class="card" style="text-align: center; color: #64748b;">No attendance records found for this semester.</div>';
                    }
                })
                .catch(function (error) {
                    console.error('Error:', error);
                    document.getElementById('attendanceList').innerHTML = '<div class="alert alert-error">Failed to load attendance</div>';
                });
        }
    </script>
</body>

</html>

            <div style="text-align: center; padding: 20px; color: #64748b;">Loading...</div>
        </div>
    </div>

    <script src="../js/main.js"></script>
    <script>
        if (!checkStudentLogin()) {}

        var student = getStudentInfo();
        document.getElementById('userName').textContent = student.name;
        document.getElementById('studentName').textContent = student.name;
        document.getElementById('studentRoll').textContent = student.roll;
        document.getElementById('studentDept').textContent = student.dept;
        document.getElementById('studentSem').textContent = student.semester;
        document.getElementById('welcomeCard').classList.add('student');

        // Load dashboard data
        loadDashboardStats();
        loadLatestAnnouncements();

        function loadDashboardStats() {
            // Get attendance stats
            fetch('../api/get-attendance.php?roll=' + student.roll)
                .then(function (res) { return res.json(); })
                .then(function (data) {
                    if (data.success && data.data.length > 0) {
                        // Calculate overall attendance
                        var totalPresent = 0;
                        var totalClasses = 0;
                        data.data.forEach(function (subject) {
                            totalPresent += parseInt(subject.present_count);
                            totalClasses += parseInt(subject.total_classes);
                        });
                        var overall = totalClasses > 0 ? Math.round((totalPresent / totalClasses) * 100) : 0;
                        document.getElementById('overallAttendance').textContent = overall + '%';
                    } else {
                        document.getElementById('overallAttendance').textContent = 'N/A';
                    }
                });

            // Get upcoming exams count
            fetch('../api/get-exams.php?dept=' + student.dept + '&semester=' + student.semester)
                .then(function (res) { return res.json(); })
                .then(function (data) {
                    if (data.success) {
                        document.getElementById('upcomingExams').textContent = data.data.length;
                    }
                });

            // Get announcements count
            fetch('../api/get-announcements.php')
                .then(function (res) { return res.json(); })
                .then(function (data) {
                    if (data.success) {
                        document.getElementById('newAnnouncements').textContent = data.data.length;
                    }
                });
        }

        function loadLatestAnnouncements() {
            fetch('../api/get-announcements.php?limit=3')
                .then(function (res) { return res.json(); })
                .then(function (data) {
                    var container = document.getElementById('announcementsList');

                    if (data.success && data.data.length > 0) {
                        var html = '';
                        data.data.forEach(function (item) {
                            var priorityClass = item.priority === 'high' ? 'high' : '';
                            var mediaIcons = '';
            if (item.attachment_url) mediaIcons += ' <i class="fas fa-paperclip"></i>';
            if (item.link_url) mediaIcons += ' <i class="fas fa-link"></i>';
                            
                            html += '<div class="announcement-item ' + priorityClass + '" onclick="window.location.href=\'announcements.html\'" style="cursor: pointer;">';
                            html += '  <div class="title">' + item.title + mediaIcons + '</div>';
                            html += '  <div class="date">' + formatDate(item.created_at) + '</div>';
                            html += '</div>';
                        });
                        container.innerHTML = html;
                    } else {
                        container.innerHTML = '<div style="padding: 20px; text-align: center; color: #64748b;">No announcements</div>';
                    }
                });
        }
    </script>
</body>

</html>

FILE: student\exams.html

<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="en">

<head>
    <meta charset="UTF-8">
    <meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0">
    <title>Exam Timetable - ICC Companion</title>
    <link rel="stylesheet" href="../css/style.css">
    <style>
        .schedule-file-card {
            background: linear-gradient(135deg, #fee2e2, #fecaca);
            border-left: 4px solid #ef4444;
            margin-bottom: 20px;
        }

        .schedule-file-card.sessional {
            background: linear-gradient(135deg, #fef3c7, #fde68a);
            border-left-color: #f59e0b;
        }
    </style>
</head>

<body>
    <!-- Header -->
    <div class="header">
        <div class="logo">
            <span class="logo-icon"><i class="fas fa-graduation-cap"></i></span>
            <div>
                <div class="college-name">ICC Companion</div>
                <div class="college-short">ICON COMMERCE COLLEGE</div>
            </div>
        </div>
        <div class="user-info">
            <span class="user-name">Welcome, <strong id="userName">Student</strong></span>
            <button class="btn-logout" onclick="logout('student')">Logout</button>
        </div>
    </div>

    <!-- Navigation -->
    <nav class="nav-menu">
        <ul>
            <li><a href="dashboard.html"><i class="fas fa-house"></i> Dashboard</a></li>
            <li><a href="subjects.html"><i class="fas fa-book-open"></i> Subjects</a></li>
            <li><a href="resources.html"><i class="fas fa-book"></i> Resources</a></li>
            <li><a href="attendance.html"><i class="fas fa-chart-bar"></i> Attendance</a></li>
            <li><a href="exams.html" class="active"><i class="fas fa-calendar"></i> Exams</a></li>
            <li><a href="routines.html"><i class="fas fa-clipboard"></i> Routines</a></li>
            <li><a href="lost-found.html"><i class="fas fa-magnifying-glass"></i> Lost & Found</a></li>
            <li><a href="announcements.html"><i class="fas fa-bullhorn"></i> Announcements</a></li>
        </ul>
    </nav>

    <!-- Main Content -->
    <div class="container">
        <h1 class="page-title"><i class="fas fa-calendar"></i> Exam Timetable</h1>

        <div style="color: #64748b; font-size: 14px; margin-bottom: 20px;">
            Showing exams for: <strong id="filterInfo">-</strong>
        </div>

        <!-- SESSIONAL SECTION -->
        <div class="card" style="border-left: 4px solid #f59e0b;">
            <h3 style="margin: 0 0 15px 0; color: #d97706;"><i class="fas fa-clipboard"></i> Sessional Examinations</h3>

            <!-- Schedule File (if uploaded) -->
            <div id="sessionalSchedule" style="display: none;"></div>

            <!-- Exam List -->
            <div id="sessionalExams">
                <div style="text-align: center; padding: 15px; color: #64748b;">Loading...</div>
            </div>
        </div>

        <!-- FINAL SECTION -->
        <div class="card" style="border-left: 4px solid #ef4444;">
            <h3 style="margin: 0 0 15px 0; color: #dc2626;">📝 Final Examinations</h3>

            <!-- Schedule File (if uploaded) -->
            <div id="finalSchedule" style="display: none;"></div>

            <!-- Exam List -->
            <div id="finalExams">
                <div style="text-align: center; padding: 15px; color: #64748b;">Loading...</div>
            </div>
        </div>
    </div>

    <script src="../js/main.js"></script>
    <script>
        if (!checkStudentLogin()) { }

        var student = getStudentInfo();
        document.getElementById('userName').textContent = student.name;
        document.getElementById('filterInfo').textContent = student.dept + ' - Semester ' + student.semester;

        loadScheduleFiles();
        loadExams();

        function loadScheduleFiles() {
            fetch('../api/get-exam-schedules.php?dept=' + student.dept + '&semester=' + student.semester)
                .then(function (res) { return res.json(); })
                .then(function (data) {
                    if (data.success) {
                        data.data.forEach(function (s) {
                            var container = document.getElementById(s.schedule_type + 'Schedule');
                            var fileExt = s.file_url.split('.').pop().toLowerCase();

                            var html = '<div style="background: #f8fafc; padding: 15px; border-radius: 8px; margin-bottom: 15px; text-align: center;">';

                            if (['jpg', 'jpeg', 'png'].includes(fileExt)) {
                                html += '<img src="../' + s.file_url + '" style="max-width: 100%; border-radius: 8px;">';
                            } else if (fileExt === 'pdf') {
                                // PDF - embed inline
                                html += '<iframe src="../' + s.file_url + '" style="width: 100%; height: 500px; border: none; border-radius: 8px;"></iframe>';
                                html += '<div style="margin-top: 10px;"><a href="../' + s.file_url + '" target="_blank" class="btn btn-sm btn-outline"><i class="fas fa-link"></i> Open in New Tab</a></div>';
                            } else {
                                html += '<a href="../' + s.file_url + '" target="_blank" class="btn btn-sm btn-primary"><i class="fas fa-download"></i> Download</a>';
                            }

                            html += '</div>';
                            container.innerHTML = html;
                            container.style.display = 'block';
                        });
                    }
                });
        }

        function loadExams() {
            var url = '../api/get-exams.php?dept=' + student.dept + '&semester=' + student.semester;

            fetch(url)
                .then(function (res) { return res.json(); })
                .then(function (data) {
                    if (data.success) {
                        var sessional = data.data.filter(function (e) { return e.exam_type === 'sessional'; });
                        var final = data.data.filter(function (e) { return e.exam_type === 'final'; });

                        renderExamList('sessionalExams', sessional);
                        renderExamList('finalExams', final);
                    }
                });
        }

        function renderExamList(containerId, exams) {
            var container = document.getElementById(containerId);

            if (exams.length === 0) {
                container.innerHTML = '<div style="text-align: center; padding: 20px; color: #64748b;">No exams scheduled yet</div>';
                return;
            }

            var html = '';
            exams.forEach(function (exam) {
                var days = daysRemaining(exam.exam_date);
                var daysText = days < 0 ? 'Done' : (days === 0 ? 'Today!' : days + ' days');
                var badgeClass = days < 0 ? '' : (days <= 3 ? 'badge-danger' : (days <= 7 ? 'badge-warning' : 'badge-success'));

                html += '<div style="display: flex; align-items: center; padding: 12px 0; border-bottom: 1px solid #e2e8f0;">';

                // Date
                html += '<div style="text-align: center; min-width: 60px; margin-right: 15px;">';
                html += '<div style="font-size: 20px; font-weight: bold; color: var(--primary);">' + new Date(exam.exam_date).getDate() + '</div>';
                html += '<div style="font-size: 11px; color: #64748b;">' + new Date(exam.exam_date).toLocaleString('en', { month: 'short' }) + '</div>';
                html += '</div>';

                // Details
                html += '<div style="flex: 1;">';
                html += '<div style="font-weight: 600;">' + exam.subject_name + '</div>';
                html += '<div style="font-size: 12px; color: #64748b;"><i class="fas fa-clock"></i> ' + formatTimeStr(exam.start_time) + ' - ' + formatTimeStr(exam.end_time) + ' | <i class="fas fa-location-dot"></i> ' + exam.room + '</div>';
                html += '</div>';

                // Days badge
                if (days >= 0) {
                    html += '<span class="badge ' + badgeClass + '">' + daysText + '</span>';
                }

                html += '</div>';
            });

            container.innerHTML = html;
        }

        function formatTimeStr(t) {
            var p = t.split(':');
            var h = parseInt(p[0]);
            var ampm = h >= 12 ? 'PM' : 'AM';
            return (h % 12 || 12) + ':' + p[1] + ' ' + ampm;
        }
    </script>
</body>

</html>

========================================================================================================================

        </ul>
    </nav>

    <!-- Main Content -->
    <div class="container">
        <h1 class="page-title"><i class="fas fa-magnifying-glass"></i> Lost & Found</h1>

        <!-- Filters -->
        <div class="card">
            <div style="display: flex; gap: 15px; flex-wrap: wrap;">
                <div class="form-group" style="margin-bottom: 0; min-width: 120px;">
                    <label for="itemType">Type</label>
                    <select id="itemType" onchange="loadItems()">
                        <option value="">All</option>
                        <option value="lost">Lost</option>
                        <option value="found">Found</option>
                    </select>
                </div>
                <div class="form-group" style="margin-bottom: 0; min-width: 140px;">
                    <label for="category">Category</label>
                    <select id="category" onchange="loadItems()">
                        <option value="">All Categories</option>
                        <option value="id-card">ID Cards</option>
                        <option value="phone">Phones</option>
                        <option value="wallet">Wallets</option>
                        <option value="books">Books</option>
                        <option value="electronics">Electronics</option>
                        <option value="other">Other</option>
                    </select>
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>

        <!-- Items Grid -->
        <div id="itemsGrid"
            style="display: grid; grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fill, minmax(280px, 1fr)); gap: 20px;">
            <div style="text-align: center; padding: 40px; color: #64748b; grid-column: 1/-1;">Loading items...</div>
        </div>
    </div>

    <script src="../js/main.js"></script>
    <script>
        // Check if logged in
        if (!checkStudentLogin()) {
            // Will redirect to login
        }

        // Get student info and display
        var student = getStudentInfo();
        document.getElementById('userName').textContent = student.name;

        // Load items
        loadItems();

        function loadItems() {
            var itemType = document.getElementById('itemType').value;
            var category = document.getElementById('category').value;

            var url = '../api/get-lost-found.php?status=active';
            if (itemType) url += '&type=' + itemType;
            if (category) url += '&category=' + category;

            fetch(url)
                .then(function (res) { return res.json(); })
                .then(function (data) {
                    var container = document.getElementById('itemsGrid');

                    if (data.success && data.data.length > 0) {
                        var html = '';

                        data.data.forEach(function (item) {
                            var typeClass = item.item_type === 'lost' ? 'badge-danger' : 'badge-success';
                            var icon = getCategoryIcon(item.category);

                            html += '<div class="item-card">';
                            // Show image if available, otherwise show icon
                            if (item.image_url) {
                                html += '  <div class="item-image" style="background: url(../' + item.image_url + ') center/cover;"></div>';
                            } else {
                                html += '  <div class="item-image">' + icon + '</div>';
                            }
                            html += '  <div class="item-details">';
                            html += '    <span class="badge ' + typeClass + '" style="margin-bottom: 8px;">' + item.item_type.toUpperCase() + '</span>';
                            html += '    <div class="item-title">' + item.title + '</div>';
                            html += '    <div class="item-location"><i class="fas fa-location-dot"></i> ' + item.location + '</div>';
                            html += '    <div style="font-size: 12px; color: #64748b; margin-bottom: 10px;"><i class="fas fa-calendar"></i> ' + formatDate(item.item_date) + '</div>';
                            if (item.description) {
                                html += '    <div style="font-size: 13px; color: #475569; margin-bottom: 10px;">' + item.description.substring(0, 100) + '</div>';
                            }
                            html += '    <div style="font-size: 12px; background: #f1f5f9; padding: 8px; border-radius: 4px;"><i class="fas fa-phone"></i> ' + item.contact_info + '</div>';
                            html += '  </div>';
                            html += '</div>';
                        });

                        container.innerHTML = html;
                    } else {
                        container.innerHTML = '<div style="text-align: center; padding: 40px; color: #64748b; grid-column: 1/-1;">No items found matching your filters.</div>';
                    }
                })
                .catch(function (error) {
                    console.error('Error:', error);
                    document.getElementById('itemsGrid').innerHTML = '<div class="alert alert-error" style="grid-column: 1/-1;">Failed to load items</div>';
                });
        }

        function getCategoryIcon(category) {
            var icons = {
                'id-card': '<i class="fas fa-id-card"></i>',
                'phone': '<i class="fas fa-mobile-screen"></i>',
                'wallet': '<i class="fas fa-wallet"></i>',
                'books': '<i class="fas fa-book"></i>',
                'electronics': '<i class="fas fa-plug"></i>',
                'other': '<i class="fas fa-box"></i>'
            };
            return icons[category] || '<i class="fas fa-box"></i>';
        }
    </script>
</body>

</html>

                    <input type="text" id="searchInput" placeholder="Search by title or subject..." oninput="loadResources()" style="width: 100%; border-radius: 8px;">
                </div>
                <div class="form-group" style="margin-bottom: 0; min-width: 150px;">
                    <select id="typeFilter" onchange="loadResources()">
                        <option value="">All Types</option>

    <!-- Text View Modal -->
    <div id="textModal" style="display: none; position: fixed; top: 0; left: 0; right: 0; bottom: 0; background: rgba(0,0,0,0.5); z-index: 1000; align-items: center; justify-content: center; padding: 20px;">
        <div class="card" style="width: 100%; max-width: 600px; margin: auto;">
            <h2 id="modalTitle" class="card-title">Resource Details</h2>
            <div id="modalContent" style="white-space: pre-wrap; margin-bottom: 20px; line-height: 1.6; color: #334155;"></div>
            <button class="btn btn-outline" onclick="closeModal()">Close</button>
        </div>
    </div>

    <script src="../js/main.js"></script>
    <script>
        if (!checkStudentLogin()) { }
        var student = getStudentInfo();
        document.getElementById('userName').textContent = student.name;
        document.getElementById('studentContext').textContent = student.dept + ' - Semester ' + student.semester;

        function loadResources() {
            var search = document.getElementById('searchInput').value.toLowerCase();
            var type = document.getElementById('typeFilter').value;
            
            var url = `../api/get-resources.php?dept=${student.dept}&semester=${student.semester}&type=${type}`;
            
            fetch(url)
                .then(res => res.json())
                .then(data => {
                    var container = document.getElementById('resourcesGrid');
                    if (data.success && data.data.length > 0) {
                        // Client-side search filtering
                        var filtered = data.data.filter(r => 
                            r.title.toLowerCase().includes(search) || 
                            (r.subject_name && r.subject_name.toLowerCase().includes(search))
                        );

                        if (filtered.length === 0) {
                            container.innerHTML = '<div style="text-align: center; padding: 40px; color: #64748b; grid-column: 1/-1;">No resources found matching your search.</div>';
                            return;
                        }

                        var html = '';
                        filtered.forEach(r => {
                            html += `
                                <div class="card resource-card">
                                    <span class="resource-type-badge type-${r.resource_type}">${r.resource_type}</span>
                                    <div style="font-size: 17px; font-weight: 600; color: var(--dark);">${r.title}</div>
                                    <div style="font-size: 13px; color: #64748b; margin-top: -5px;">${r.subject_name || 'General Resource'}</div>
                                    
                                    <div class="resource-meta">
                                        <span><i class="fas fa-calendar"></i> ${formatDate(r.created_at)}</span>
                                    </div>

                                    <div style="margin-top: auto; padding-top: 15px;">
                                        ${r.file_url ? `<a href="../${r.file_url}" target="_blank" class="btn btn-primary btn-sm" style="display:inline-block; margin-right:5px;"><i class="fas fa-download"></i> Download</a>` : ''}
                                        ${r.link_url ? `<a href="${r.link_url}" target="_blank" class="btn btn-outline btn-sm" style="display:inline-block;"><i class="fas fa-link"></i> View Link</a>` : ''}
                                        ${r.content_text ? `<button onclick="viewText('${r.title}', '${encodeURIComponent(r.content_text)}')" class="btn btn-outline btn-sm"><i class="fas fa-book-open"></i> Read Text</button>` : ''}
                                    </div>
                                </div>
                            `;
                        });
                        container.innerHTML = html;
                    } else {
                        container.innerHTML = '<div style="text-align: center; padding: 40px; color: #64748b; grid-column: 1/-1;">No resources available for your semester yet.</div>';
                    }
                });
        }

        function viewText(title, text) {
            document.getElementById('modalTitle').textContent = title;
            document.getElementById('modalContent').textContent = decodeURIComponent(text);
            document.getElementById('textModal').style.display = 'flex';
        }

        function closeModal() {
            document.getElementById('textModal').style.display = 'none';
        }

        // Initialize
        loadResources();
    </script>
</body>

</html>
